Key questions:
1. How does the queue command decide what to show? Where does the "planned" count come from vs "subjective" count?
2. What controls whether subjective_assessment items appear in the queue?
3. How are items counted as "planned" — is it based on cluster membership or queue_order?
4. Could the issue be that cluster members were resolved/disappeared but their IDs are still in queue_order, making the queue think there are 16 planned items, but those items can't be displayed?

Search thoroughly in:
- desloppify/app/commands/plan/ for queue display logic
- desloppify/engine/_plan/ for queue building, reconciliation, subjective assessment insertion
- Look for "subjective" references to understand when they get added to queue_order
- Look for "planned" counting logic
- Look for reconciliation logic that should remove disappeared items from queue_order

Be very thorough - this is a logic bug investigation.

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from .work_queue_helpers import _ALL_STATUSES, ATTEST_EXAMPLE, _build_subjective_items, _scope_matches
     8→from .work_queue_ranking import (
     9→    _build_finding_items,
    10→    _choose_fallback_tier,
    11→    _item_explain,
    12→    _item_sort_key,
    13→    _tier_counts,
    14→    group_queue_items,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    19→class QueueBuildOptions:
    20→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    21→
    22→    tier: int | None = None
    23→    count: int | None = 1
    24→    scan_path: str | None = None
    25→    scope: str | None = None
    26→    status: str = "open"
    27→    include_subjective: bool = True
    28→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    29→    chronic: bool = False
    30→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    31→    explain: bool = False
    32→
    33→
    34→def build_work_queue(
    35→    state: dict,
    36→    *,
    37→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    38→    **legacy_kwargs: object,
    39→) -> dict[str, object]:
    40→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    41→    base_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    42→    resolved_options = QueueBuildOptions(
    43→        tier=legacy_kwargs.pop("tier", base_options.tier),
    44→        count=legacy_kwargs.pop("count", base_options.count),
    45→        scan_path=legacy_kwargs.pop("scan_path", base_options.scan_path),
    46→        scope=legacy_kwargs.pop("scope", base_options.scope),
    47→        status=legacy_kwargs.pop("status", base_options.status),
    48→        include_subjective=legacy_kwargs.pop(
    49→            "include_subjective", base_options.include_subjective
    50→        ),
    51→        subjective_threshold=legacy_kwargs.pop(
    52→            "subjective_threshold", base_options.subjective_threshold
    53→        ),
    54→        chronic=legacy_kwargs.pop("chronic", base_options.chronic),
    55→        no_tier_fallback=legacy_kwargs.pop(
    56→            "no_tier_fallback", base_options.no_tier_fallback
    57→        ),
    58→        explain=legacy_kwargs.pop("explain", base_options.explain),
    59→    )
    60→    if legacy_kwargs:
    61→        unknown = next(iter(legacy_kwargs))
    62→        raise TypeError(
    63→            f"build_work_queue() got an unexpected keyword argument '{unknown}'"
    64→        )
    65→
    66→    status = resolved_options.status
    67→    if status not in _ALL_STATUSES:
    68→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    69→    try:
    70→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    71→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    72→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    73→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    74→
    75→    finding_items = _build_finding_items(
    76→        state,
    77→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    78→        status_filter=status,
    79→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    80→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    84→    if resolved_options.include_subjective and status in {"open", "all"} and not resolved_options.chronic:
    85→        subjective_items = _build_subjective_items(
    86→            state,
    87→            state.get("findings", {}),
    88→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
    89→        )
    90→        for item in subjective_items:
    91→            if _scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
    92→                all_items.append(item)
    93→
    94→    all_items.sort(key=_item_sort_key)
    95→    counts = _tier_counts(all_items)
    96→
    97→    requested_tier = int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
    98→    selected_tier = requested_tier
    99→    fallback_reason = None
   100→    filtered = all_items
   101→
   102→    if requested_tier is not None:
   103→        filtered = [
   104→            item
   105→            for item in all_items
   106→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   107→        ]
   108→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   109→            chosen = _choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   110→            if chosen is not None:
   111→                selected_tier = chosen
   112→                filtered = [
   113→                    item
   114→                    for item in all_items
   115→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   116→                ]
   117→                fallback_reason = (
   118→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   119→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   120→                )
   121→        elif not filtered:
   122→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   123→
   124→    total = len(filtered)
   125→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   126→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   127→
   128→    if resolved_options.explain:
   129→        for item in filtered:
   130→            item["explain"] = _item_explain(item)
   131→
   132→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   133→    return {
   134→        "items": filtered,
   135→        "total": total,
   136→        "tier_counts": counts,
   137→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   138→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   139→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   140→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   141→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   142→    }
   143→
   144→
   145→__all__ = ["ATTEST_EXAMPLE", "QueueBuildOptions", "build_work_queue", "group_queue_items"]
   146→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→
     7→from .plan_common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
     8→from .work_queue_helpers import (
     9→    _is_review_finding,
    10→    _is_subjective_finding,
    11→    _primary_command_for_finding,
    12→    _review_finding_weight,
    13→    _scope_matches,
    14→    _slugify,
    15→    _status_matches,
    16→    _subjective_strict_scores,
    17→    _supported_fixers_for_item,
    18→)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def _build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    state_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.state")
    37→
    38→    scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    39→    subjective_scores = _subjective_strict_scores(state)
    40→    out: list[dict] = []
    41→
    42→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    43→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    44→            continue
    45→        if not _status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    46→            continue
    47→        if chronic and not (
    48→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    49→        ):
    50→            continue
    51→
    52→        item = dict(finding)
    53→        item["id"] = finding_id
    54→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    55→        item["is_review"] = _is_review_finding(item)
    56→        item["is_subjective"] = _is_subjective_finding(item)
    57→        if item["is_review"]:
    58→            item["effective_tier"] = 1
    59→        else:
    60→            item["effective_tier"] = 4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    61→        item["review_weight"] = _review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    62→        subjective_score = None
    63→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    64→            detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    65→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    66→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or _slugify(dim_name)
    67→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0))
    68→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    69→        supported_fixers = _supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    70→        item["primary_command"] = _primary_command_for_finding(
    71→            item,
    72→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    73→        )
    74→
    75→        if not _scope_matches(item, scope):
    76→            continue
    77→        out.append(item)
    78→
    79→    return out
    80→
    81→
    82→def _item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    83→    if item.get("is_review"):
    84→        # Review queue is always highest priority in `next`.
    85→        return (
    86→            0,
    87→            -float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0),
    88→            CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    93→        return (
    94→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    95→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    96→            _subjective_score_value(item),
    97→            item.get("id", ""),
    98→        )
    99→
   100→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   101→    return (
   102→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   103→        0,
   104→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   105→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   106→        item.get("id", ""),
   107→    )
   108→
   109→
   110→def _item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   111→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   112→    if item.get("is_review"):
   113→        return {
   114→            "kind": "finding",
   115→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   116→            "confidence": item.get("confidence", "low"),
   117→            "review_weight": float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0),
   118→            "id": item.get("id", ""),
   119→            "policy": (
   120→                "Open review findings are always ranked first and shown before "
   121→                "mechanical or synthetic subjective queue items."
   122→            ),
   123→            "ranking_factors": [
   124→                "review_priority",
   125→                "review_weight desc",
   126→                "confidence asc",
   127→                "id asc",
   128→            ],
   129→        }
   130→
   131→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   132→        return {
   133→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   134→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   135→            "subjective_score": _subjective_score_value(item),
   136→            "policy": (
   137→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   138→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   139→            ),
   140→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   141→        }
   142→
   143→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   144→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   145→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   146→    ranking_factors = (
   147→        ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   148→        if is_subjective
   149→        else ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   150→    )
   151→    explain = {
   152→        "kind": "finding",
   153→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   154→        "confidence": confidence,
   155→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   156→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   157→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   158→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   159→    }
   160→    if is_subjective:
   161→        explain["policy"] = (
   162→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   163→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   164→        )
   165→        explain["subjective_score"] = _subjective_score_value(item)
   166→    return explain
   167→
   168→
   169→def _tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   170→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   171→    for item in items:
   172→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   173→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   174→    return counts
   175→
   176→
   177→def _choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   178→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   179→    if not available:
   180→        return None
   181→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   182→
   183→
   184→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   185→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   186→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   187→    for item in items:
   188→        if group == "file":
   189→            key = item.get("file", "")
   190→        elif group == "detector":
   191→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   192→        elif group == "tier":
   193→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   194→        else:
   195→            key = "items"
   196→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   197→    return grouped
   198→
   199→
   200→__all__ = [
   201→    "_build_finding_items",
   202→    "_choose_fallback_tier",
   203→    "_item_explain",
   204→    "_item_sort_key",
   205→    "_tier_counts",
   206→    "group_queue_items",
   207→]
   208→
```

```
    40→def _serialize_item(item: dict) -> dict:
    41→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    42→    serialized = {
    43→        "id": item.get("id"),
    44→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    45→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    46→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    47→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    48→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    49→        "file": item.get("file"),
    50→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    51→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    52→        "status": item.get("status"),
    53→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    54→    }
    55→    explain = item.get("explain")
    56→    if explain is not None:
    57→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    58→    return serialized
    59→
    60→
    61→def _scorecard_subjective(
    62→    state_or_dim_scores: dict,
    63→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    66→    state = state_or_dim_scores
    67→    if dim_scores is None:
    68→        dim_scores = state_or_dim_scores
    69→        state = {"dimension_scores": dim_scores}
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    73→        state,
    74→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    79→    state_or_dim_scores: dict,
    80→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    81→    *,
    82→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    83→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    84→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    85→    if dim_scores is None:
    86→        dim_scores = state_or_dim_scores
    87→        state = {"dimension_scores": dim_scores}
    88→    else:
    89→        state = state_or_dim_scores
    90→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    91→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    92→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    93→            continue
    94→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    95→        if strict_val < threshold:
    96→            low.append((str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")), strict_val, int(entry.get("issues", 0))))
    97→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    98→    return low
    99→

   241→def _build_query_payload(queue: dict, items: list[dict], *, command: str, narrative: dict | None) -> dict:
   242→    serialized = [_serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   243→    return {
   244→        "command": command,
   245→        "items": serialized,
   246→        "queue": {
   247→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
   248→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
   249→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
   250→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
   251→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
   252→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   253→        },
   254→        "narrative": narrative,
   255→    }
   256→
   257→
   258→def _write_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, item_count: int) -> bool:
   259→    try:
   260→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   261→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   262→    except OSError as e:
   263→        payload["output_error"] = str(e)
   264→        print(colorize(f"Could not write to {output_file}: {e}", "red"))
   265→        return False
   266→    return True
   267→
   268→
   269→def _emit_non_terminal_output(output_format: str, payload: dict, items: list[dict]) -> bool:
   270→    renderers = {
   271→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   272→        "md": lambda: print(_render_markdown(items)),
   273→    }
   274→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   275→    if renderer is None:

   277→    renderer()
   278→    return True

   350→
   351→def _render_followup_nudges(
   352→    state: dict,
   353→    dim_scores: dict,
   354→    findings_scoped: dict,
   355→    *,
   356→    strict_score: float | None,
   357→    target_strict_score: float,
   358→) -> None:
   359→    subjective_threshold = coerce_target_score(target_strict_score, fallback=95.0)
   360→    subjective_entries = _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   361→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   362→        state,
   363→        subjective_entries,
   364→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   365→        max_quality_items=3,
   366→        max_integrity_items=5,
   367→    )
   368→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   369→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   370→    if strict_score is not None:
   371→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   372→        if gap > 0:
   373→            print(
   374→                colorize(
   375→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   376→                    "cyan",
   377→                )
   378→            )
   379→        else:
   380→            print(
   381→                colorize(
   382→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   383→                    "green",
   384→                )
   385→            )
   386→    if low_assessed:
   387→        print(
   388→            colorize(
   389→                f"\n  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): {followup.rendered}",
   390→                "cyan",
   391→            )
   392→        )
   393→        print(
   394→            colorize(
   395→                "  Next command to improve subjective scores: "
   396→                f"{followup.command}",
   397→                "dim",
   398→            )
   399→        )
   400→
   401→    if followup.integrity_lines:
   402→        print()
   403→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   404→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
   405→
   406→    coverage_open, coverage_reasons, holistic_reasons = _subjective_coverage_breakdown(findings_scoped)
   407→    holistic_open = sum(holistic_reasons.values())
   408→    if unassessed_subjective or holistic_open > 0:
   409→        bits: list[str] = []
   410→        if unassessed_subjective:
   411→            bits.append("unassessed subjective dimensions")
   412→        if holistic_open > 0:
   413→            bits.append("holistic review stale/missing")
   414→        gap_label = " + ".join(bits)
   415→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective integrity gap: {gap_label}", "yellow"))
   416→        print(colorize("  Priority: `desloppify review --prepare --holistic --refresh`", "dim"))
   417→        print(colorize("  Then import and rerun `desloppify scan` to refresh strict score.", "dim"))
   418→        if unassessed_subjective:
   419→            rendered = ", ".join(name for name in unassessed_subjective[:3])
   420→            if len(unassessed_subjective) > 3:
   421→                rendered = f"{rendered}, +{len(unassessed_subjective) - 3} more"
   422→            print(colorize(f"  Unassessed (0% placeholder): {rendered}", "dim"))
   423→
   424→    open_review = [
   425→        finding
   426→        for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   427→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   428→    ]
   429→    if low_assessed and open_review:
   430→        print(
   431→            colorize(
   432→                "  Subjective bottleneck: prioritize `desloppify issues` to move elegance scores.",
   433→                "yellow",
   434→            )
   435→        )
   436→
   437→    if coverage_open > 0:
   438→        reason_parts = []
   439→        if coverage_reasons.get("changed", 0) > 0:
   440→            reason_parts.append(f"{coverage_reasons['changed']} changed")
   441→        if coverage_reasons.get("unreviewed", 0) > 0:
   442→            reason_parts.append(f"{coverage_reasons['unreviewed']} unreviewed")
   443→        reason_text = ", ".join(reason_parts) if reason_parts else "stale/unreviewed"
   444→        suffix = "file" if coverage_open == 1 else "files"
   445→        print(colorize(f"  Subjective coverage debt: {coverage_open} {suffix} ({reason_text})", "cyan"))
   446→        if holistic_open > 0:
   447→            print(colorize(f"  Includes {holistic_open} holistic stale/missing signal(s).", "yellow"))
   448→        print(colorize("  Triage: `desloppify show subjective_review --status open`", "dim"))
   449→
   450→    if open_review:
   451→        uninvestigated = sum(1 for finding in open_review if not finding.get("detail", {}).get("investigation"))
   452→        suffix = f" ({uninvestigated} uninvestigated)" if uninvestigated else ""
   453→        print(colorize(f"  Also: {len(open_review)} review findings open{suffix}. Run `desloppify issues`.", "cyan"))
   454→    elif low_assessed or unassessed_subjective or coverage_open > 0:
   455→        print(colorize("  Then import review output and rerun `desloppify scan` to refresh subjective scores.", "dim"))
   456→
   457→
   458→def cmd_next(args) -> None:
   459→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
   460→    sp = state_path(args)
   461→    state = state_mod.load_state(sp)
   462→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   463→        return
   464→
   465→    stale_warning = utils_mod.check_tool_staleness(state)
   466→    if stale_warning:
   467→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   468→
   469→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   470→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   471→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   472→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   473→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   474→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   475→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   476→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
   477→
   478→    config = getattr(args, "_config", {}) or {}
   479→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   480→
   481→    queue = build_work_queue(
   482→        state,
   483→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   484→            tier=tier,
   485→            count=count,
   486→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   487→            scope=scope,
   488→            status=status,
   489→            include_subjective=True,
   490→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   491→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   492→            explain=explain,
   493→        ),
   494→    )
   495→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   496→
   497→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   498→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   499→    narrative = compute_narrative(state, lang=lang_name, command="next")
   500→
   501→    payload = _build_query_payload(queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative)
   502→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   503→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   504→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   505→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   506→        state,
   507→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   508→    )
   509→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   510→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   511→    ]
   512→    _write_query(payload)
   513→
   514→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   515→    if output_file:
   516→        if _write_output_file(output_file, payload, len(items)):
   517→            return
   518→        raise SystemExit(1)
   519→
   520→    if _emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   521→        return
   522→
   523→    _render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   524→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   525→    if _show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   526→        return
   527→
   528→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   529→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   530→        state.get("findings", {}),
   531→        state.get("scan_path"),
   532→    )
   533→    _render_terminal_items(items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain)
   534→    _render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   535→    _render_followup_nudges(
   536→        state,
   537→        dim_scores,
   538→        findings_scoped,
   539→        strict_score=strict_score,
   540→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   541→    )
   542→    print()
   543→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     6→import importlib
     7→import re
     8→
     9→_ALL_STATUSES = {"open", "fixed", "wontfix", "false_positive", "auto_resolved", "all"}
    10→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    11→    "I have actually improved [WHAT YOU IMPROVED EXPLICITLY] enough "
    12→    "to honestly justify a score of [SCORE], and I am not gaming the score."
    13→)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    17→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    18→
    19→
    20→def _is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    21→    detector = item.get("detector")
    22→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    23→        return True
    24→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    25→        return True
    26→    return False
    27→
    28→
    29→def _is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    30→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    31→
    32→
    33→def _review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    34→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    35→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    36→    weight_by_confidence = {
    37→        "high": 1.0,
    38→        "medium": 0.7,
    39→        "low": 0.3,
    40→    }
    41→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    42→    if item.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
    43→        weight *= 10.0
    44→    return float(weight)
    45→
    46→
    47→def _scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    48→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    49→    if not scope:
    50→        return True
    51→
    52→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    53→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    54→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    55→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    56→    dimension = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension_name", "")
    57→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    58→
    59→    if "*" in scope:
    60→        return any(
    61→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    62→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    63→        )
    64→
    65→    if "::" in scope:
    66→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    67→
    68→    lowered = scope.lower()
    69→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    70→        return lowered in item_id.lower() or lowered in dimension.lower() or lowered in summary.lower()
    71→
    72→    return (
    73→        detector == scope
    74→        or filepath == scope
    75→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
    76→    )
    77→
    78→
    79→def _slugify(text: str) -> str:
    80→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
    81→
    82→
    83→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    84→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid Elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    85→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    86→    target = cleaned.lower()
    87→    scoring_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.scoring")
    88→
    89→    for dim_key, label in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    90→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    91→            return str(dim_key)
    92→    return _slugify(cleaned)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    96→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
    97→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    98→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    99→    return {
   100→        cleaned.lower(),
   101→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   102→        _slugify(cleaned),
   103→        canonical.lower(),
   104→        _slugify(canonical),
   105→    }
   106→
   107→
   108→def _supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   109→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   110→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   111→    if not lang:
   112→        return None
   113→
   114→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   115→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   116→        return None
   117→
   118→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   119→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   120→        return None
   121→
   122→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   123→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   124→        return None
   125→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   126→
   127→
   128→def _primary_command_for_finding(item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None) -> str:
   129→    registry_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.registry")
   130→    subjective_integrity_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.subjective_review_integrity")
   131→
   132→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   133→    meta = registry_mod.DETECTORS.get(detector)
   134→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   135→        available_fixers = [
   136→            fixer for fixer in meta.fixers
   137→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   138→        ]
   139→        if available_fixers:
   140→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   141→    if detector == "review":
   142→        return "desloppify issues"
   143→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   144→        if subjective_integrity_mod.is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   145→            return "desloppify review --prepare --holistic --refresh"
   146→        return "desloppify show subjective_review --status open"
   147→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   148→
   149→
   150→def _subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   151→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   152→    if not dim_scores:
   153→        return {}
   154→
   155→    scorecard_projection_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.output.scorecard_projection")
   156→    entries = scorecard_projection_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   157→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   158→    for entry in entries:
   159→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   160→        if not name:
   161→            continue
   162→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   163→        [REDACTED](name)
   164→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   165→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   166→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   167→            if not key:
   168→                continue
   169→            aliases.add(key)
   170→            aliases.add(_slugify(key))
   171→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   172→        aliases.add(_slugify(dim_key))
   173→        for alias in aliases:
   174→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   175→    return scores
   176→
   177→
   178→def _build_subjective_items(state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0) -> list[dict]:
   179→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   180→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   181→    if not dim_scores:
   182→        return []
   183→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   184→
   185→    scorecard_projection_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.output.scorecard_projection")
   186→    subjective_integrity_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.subjective_review_integrity")
   187→
   188→    subjective_entries = scorecard_projection_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   189→        state, dim_scores=dim_scores
   190→    )
   191→    if not subjective_entries:
   192→        return []
   193→    unassessed_dims = {
   194→        str(name).strip()
   195→        for name in subjective_integrity_mod.unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   196→    }
   197→
   198→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   199→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   200→    for finding in findings.values():
   201→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   202→            continue
   203→        dim_key = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   204→        if not dim_key:
   205→            continue
   206→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   207→
   208→    items: list[dict] = []
   209→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   210→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   211→        if not name:
   212→            continue
   213→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   214→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   215→            continue
   216→
   217→        [REDACTED](name)
   218→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   219→        cli_keys = [str(key).strip().lower() for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []) if str(key).strip()]
   220→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   221→        aliases.update(_slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   222→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   223→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   224→            name in unassessed_dims
   225→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   226→        )
   227→        if is_unassessed:
   228→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare --holistic --refresh"
   229→        else:
   230→            if open_review > 0:
   231→                primary_command = "desloppify issues"
   232→            elif cli_keys:
   233→                primary_command = (
   234→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   235→                    + ",".join(cli_keys)
   236→                )
   237→            else:
   238→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   239→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%)"
   240→        items.append(
   241→            {
   242→                "id": f"subjective::{_slugify(dim_key)}",
   243→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   244→                "file": ".",
   245→                "tier": 4,
   246→                "effective_tier": 4,
   247→                "confidence": "medium",
   248→                "summary": summary,
   249→                "detail": {
   250→                    "dimension_name": name,
   251→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   252→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   253→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   254→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   255→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   256→                },
   257→                "status": "open",
   258→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   259→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   260→            }
   261→        )
   262→    return items
   263→
   264→
   265→__all__ = [
   266→    "_ALL_STATUSES",
   267→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   268→    "_build_subjective_items",
   269→    "_canonical_subjective_dimension_key",
   270→    "_is_review_finding",
   271→    "_is_subjective_finding",
   272→    "_primary_command_for_finding",
```

```
1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .plan_types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from .work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     7→
     8→
     9→def get_next_items(
    10→    state: PlanState,
    11→    tier: int | None = None,
    12→    count: int = 1,
    13→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    14→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    15→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    16→
    17→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    18→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    19→    """
    20→    result = build_work_queue(
    21→        state,
    22→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    23→            tier=tier,
    24→            count=count,
    25→            scan_path=scan_path,
    26→            status="open",
    27→            include_subjective=False,
    28→            no_tier_fallback=True,
    29→        ),
    30→    )
    31→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    32→
    33→
    34→def get_next_item(
    35→    state: PlanState,
    36→    tier: int | None = None,
    37→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    38→) -> PlanItem | None:
    39→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    40→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    41→    return items[0] if items else None
    42→
```

```
1→"""Markdown plan rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict

     8→

    12→
    13→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    14→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    15→    schema_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.state_internal.schema")
    16→
    17→    overall_score = schema_mod.get_overall_score(state)
    18→    objective_score = schema_mod.get_objective_score(state)
    19→    strict_score = schema_mod.get_strict_score(state)
    20→
    21→    if (
    22→        overall_score is not None
    23→        and objective_score is not None
    24→        and strict_score is not None
    25→    ):
    26→        header_score = (
    27→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    28→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    29→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    30→        )
    31→    elif overall_score is not None:
    32→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    33→    else:
    34→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    35→
    36→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    37→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    38→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    39→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→
    41→    lines = [
    42→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    43→        "",
    44→        f"{header_score} | "
    45→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    46→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    49→        "",
    50→    ]
    51→
    52→    if total_files:
    53→        utils_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.utils")
    54→
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < utils_mod.LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n")
    61→
    62→    return lines
    63→
    64→
    65→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    67→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    68→    if not dim_scores:
    69→        return []
    70→
    71→    lines = [
    72→        "## Health by Dimension",
    73→        "",
    74→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    75→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    76→    ]
    77→    registry_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.registry")
    78→    scoring_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.scoring")
    79→
    80→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    82→    subjective_display_names = {
    83→        display.lower() for display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    87→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    88→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    89→            return True
    90→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    91→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    92→
    93→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    94→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    95→        if not ds:
    96→            continue
    97→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    99→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   100→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   101→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   102→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   103→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   104→        action = registry_mod.dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   105→        lines.append(
   106→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   107→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    scorecard_projection_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.output.scorecard_projection")
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_projection_mod.scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds)
   114→        for name, ds in scorecard_rows
   115→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   116→    ]
   117→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   118→
   119→    # Keep backward compatibility for custom dimensions that do not appear in
   120→    # scorecard.png by showing them in the main table section.
   121→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   122→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   123→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   124→            continue
   125→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   126→            continue
   127→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   128→        rendered_names.add(name)
   129→
   130→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   131→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   132→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   133→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   134→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   135→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   136→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   137→        action = registry_mod.dimension_action_type(name)
   138→        lines.append(
   139→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   140→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   141→        )
   142→
   143→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   144→        (name, ds)
   145→        for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower())
   146→        if (
   147→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   148→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   150→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   151→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   152→        )
   153→    ]
   154→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   155→
   156→    if subjective_rows:
   157→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   158→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   159→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   160→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   161→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   162→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   163→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   164→            lines.append(
   165→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   166→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   167→            )
   168→
   169→    lines.append("")
   170→    return lines
   171→
   172→
   173→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   174→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   175→    work_queue_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.work_queue")
   176→
   177→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   178→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   179→    raw_target = (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95) if isinstance(state, dict) else 95
   180→    try:
   181→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   182→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   183→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   184→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   185→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   186→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   187→
   188→    queue = work_queue_mod.build_work_queue(
   189→        queue_state,
   190→        options=work_queue_mod.QueueBuildOptions(
   191→            count=None,
   192→            scan_path=scan_path,
   193→            status="open",
   194→            include_subjective=True,
   195→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   196→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   197→        ),
   198→    )
   199→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   200→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   201→    for item in open_items:
   202→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   203→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   204→
   205→    lines: list[str] = []
   206→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   207→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   208→        if not tier_files:
   209→            continue
   210→
   211→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   212→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   213→        lines.extend([
   214→            "---",
   215→            f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   216→            "",
   217→        ])
   218→
   219→        sorted_files = sorted(tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0]))
   220→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   221→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   222→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   223→            lines.append("")
   224→            for item in file_items:
   225→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   226→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   227→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   228→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   229→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   230→                    continue
   231→
   232→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   233→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   234→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   235→            lines.append("")
   236→
   237→    return lines
   238→
   239→
   240→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   241→    lines: list[str] = []
   242→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   243→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   244→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   245→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   246→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   247→        addressed = total - open_count
   248→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   249→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   250→        lines.append(
   251→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   252→        )
   253→    lines.append("")
   254→    return lines
   255→
   256→
   257→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   258→    addressed = [finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"]
   259→    if not addressed:
   260→        return []
   261→
   262→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   263→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   264→    for finding in addressed:
   265→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   266→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   267→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   268→
   269→    wontfix = [finding for finding in addressed if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")]
   270→    if wontfix:
   271→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   272→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   273→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   274→
   275→    lines.append("")
   276→    return lines
   277→
   278→
   279→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState) -> str:
   280→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
   281→    findings = state["findings"]
   282→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   283→
   284→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   285→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   286→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   287→    lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   288→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   289→
   290→    return "\n".join(lines)
   291→
```

{item_id}\")\n\n# Check if findings in queue have specific statuses\nqueue_finding_statuses = {}\nfor qid in queue_order:\n    if qid in findings:\n        status = findings[qid].get('status', 'unknown')\n        queue_finding_statuses[status] = queue_finding_statuses.get(status, 0) + 1\n\nprint(f\"\\nStatuses of queue items that exist in findings:\")\nfor status, count in queue_finding_statuses.items():\n    print(f\"  {status}: {count}\")\n\n# Count subjective items\nprint(f\"\\n=== SUBJECTIVE ITEMS ===\")\nsubjective_items = {k: v for k, v in findings.items() if v.get('detector') in ['subjective_assessment', 'subjective_review', 'review']}\nprint(f\"Total subjective-related findings: {len(subjective_items)}\")\n\nsubjective_open = sum(1 for v in subjective_items.values() if v.get('status') == 'open')\nprint(f\"Open subjective-related findings: {subjective_open}\")\n\n# List some of them\nprint(\"\\nFirst 10 subjective findings:\")\nfor item_id, finding in list(subjective_items.items())[:10]:\n    detector = finding.get('detector')\n    status = finding.get('status')\n    print(f\"  {item_id} | {detector} | {status}\")\nEOF\n"

```
   106→    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
```

```
usage: desloppify plan [-h] [--state STATE] [--output FILE]
                       {show,queue,reset,reorder,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,resolve,focus,cluster,triage,scan-gate,commit-log}
                       ...

Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-issue annotations.
Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.

positional arguments:
  {show,queue,reset,reorder,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,resolve,focus,cluster,triage,scan-gate,commit-log}
    show                Show plan metadata summary
    queue               Compact table of upcoming queue items
    reset               Reset plan to empty
    reorder             Reposition issues in the queue
    describe            Set augmented description
    note                Set note on issues
    skip                Skip issues: temporary (default), --permanent
                        (wontfix), or --false-positive
    unskip              Bring skipped issues back to queue (reopens
                        permanent/fp in state)
    reopen              Reopen resolved issues and move back to queue
    resolve             Mark issues as fixed (shows score movement + next
                        step)
    focus               Set or clear active cluster focus
    cluster             Manage issue clusters
    triage              Staged triage workflow for review issues
    scan-gate           Check or skip the scan requirement for workflow items
    commit-log          Track commits and resolved issues for PR updates

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --state STATE         Path to state file
  --output FILE         Write to file instead of stdout (refuses to overwrite
                        existing files)

typical workflow:
  desloppify scan                       # detect issues
  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related issues
  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
  desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed

patterns (used by reorder, skip, resolve, describe, note, etc.):
  Patterns match issues by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"

subcommands:
```

```
   461→    state = state_mod.load_state(sp)

   463→        return
   464→
   465→    stale_warning = utils_mod.check_tool_staleness(state)
   466→    if stale_warning:

   468→
   469→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   470→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   471→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   472→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   473→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   474→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   475→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   476→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
   477→
   478→    config = getattr(args, "_config", {}) or {}
   479→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   480→
   481→    queue = build_work_queue(
   482→        state,
   483→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   484→            tier=tier,
   485→            count=count,
   486→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   487→            scope=scope,
   488→            status=status,
   489→            include_subjective=True,
```

```
def _serialize_item(item: dict) -> dict:
    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    serialized = {
        "id": item.get("id"),
        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
        "tier": item.get("tier"),
        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
        "detector": item.get("detector"),
        "file": item.get("file"),
        "summary": item.get("summary"),
        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
        "status": item.get("status"),
        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    }
    explain = item.get("explain")
    if explain is not None:
        serialized["explain"] = explain
    return serialized
```

```
    """Write structured query output to .desloppify/query.json.

def _cache_once(fn: Callable[[], tuple[str, ...]]) -> Callable[[], tuple[str, ...]]:
    """Cache a zero-arg function once while preserving cache_clear for tests."""
    cached = lru_cache(maxsize=1)(fn)
    return cached
```

```
"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state."""

from ..core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
from .. import plan as plan_mod
from .. import state as state_mod
from ..utils import colorize
from ..utils import safe_write_text
from ._helpers import require_completed_scan, state_path


def cmd_plan_output(args) -> None:
    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    sp = state_path(args)
    state = state_mod.load_state(sp)

    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return

    plan_md = plan_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"

    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    if output:
        try:
            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
            print(colorize(f"Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
        except OSError as e:
            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    else:
        print(plan_md)
        print(colorize(f"\nNext command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
```

```
"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state."""

from ..core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
from .. import plan as plan_mod
from .. import state as state_mod
from ..utils import colorize
from ..utils import safe_write_text
from ._helpers import require_completed_scan, state_path


def cmd_plan_output(args) -> None:
    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    sp = state_path(args)
    state = state_mod.load_state(sp)

    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return

    plan_md = plan_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"

    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    if output:
        try:
            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
            print(colorize(f"Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
        except OSError as e:
            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    else:
        print(plan_md)
        print(colorize(f"\nNext command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
```

```
    23→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    24→    if len(text) <= width:

    26→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"

    29→def _print_queue_header(
    30→    *,
    31→    items: list[dict],

    38→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    39→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    # Count subjective items in the visible list

    48→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())

   104→def _render_cluster_banner(item: dict, position: int, new_ids: set[str]) -> None:
   105→    """Render a collapsed cluster as a visually distinct banner."""
   106→    name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   107→    member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   108→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   109→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(name, action_type)
   110→    new_in_cluster = sum(
   111→        1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   112→        if m.get("id") in new_ids

   114→    new_tag = f"  (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   115→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   116→    command = item.get("primary_command", "")
   117→
   118→    label = f"{position}. {type_label} — {member_count} items{new_tag}"
   119→    width = max(len(label) + 4, 50)
   120→    bar = "─" * width
   121→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "dim"))
   122→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "bold"))
   123→    print(colorize(f"     Cluster: {name}", "dim"))
   124→    if summary:
   125→        print(colorize(f"     {summary}", "dim"))
   126→    if command:
   127→        print(colorize(f"     Run:      {command}", "cyan"))
   128→    print(colorize(f"     Drill in: desloppify plan queue --cluster {name}", "dim"))
   129→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "dim"))
   130→
   131→
   132→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
   133→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   134→    _new = new_ids or set()
   135→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   136→        pos = str(idx)
   137→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   138→
   139→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   140→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   141→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   142→            conf_str = "—"
   143→            detector = "planning"
   144→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   145→            cluster_name = ""
   146→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   147→            conf_str = "—"
   148→            detector = "workflow"
   149→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   150→            cluster_name = ""
   151→        elif kind == "cluster":
   152→            # Clusters are rendered as banners, not table rows
   153→            continue
   154→        else:
   155→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   156→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   157→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   158→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   159→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   160→
   161→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   162→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   163→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   164→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   165→    return rows
   166→
   167→
   168→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   169→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   170→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   171→    state = runtime.state
   172→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   176→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   177→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   178→
   179→    plan = load_plan()
   180→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   181→
   182→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
   183→    if not cluster_filter:
   184→        active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   185→        if active_cluster:
   186→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   187→
   188→    queue = build_work_queue(
   189→        state,
   190→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   191→            count=None,
   192→            status="open",
   193→            include_subjective=True,
   194→            plan=plan,
   195→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   196→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   197→        ),
   198→    )
   199→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   200→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   201→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   202→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   203→
   204→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   205→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   206→    # Merge review-based new issue IDs (since last triage)
   207→    review_new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   208→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   209→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   210→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   211→
   212→    if sort_by == "recent":
   213→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   214→
   215→    _print_queue_header(
   216→        items=items,
   217→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   218→        plan=plan,
   219→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    if not items:
   223→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    # Determine which items to show
   227→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   228→
   229→    # Render cluster banners first, then remaining items in the table
   230→    print()
   231→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   232→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   233→            _render_cluster_banner(item, idx, new_ids)
   234→
   235→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   236→    if rows:
   237→        headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   238→        widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   239→        print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
```

FOUND IT! Lines 42-56 are the key:

```python
# Count subjective items in the visible list
subjective = sum(
    1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
)

# Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
```

So the queue headline counts:
- `queue_total` = non_skipped items  
- `plan_ordered` = items from queue_order that aren't skipped
- `skipped` = plan.skipped
- `subjective` = items with kind == "subjective_dimension"

The question is: WHERE DO THE SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION ITEMS COME FROM? They're built by `build_work_queue`. Let me check that function to understand why it's adding "20 subjective" items:

```
    64→    scope: str | None = None
    65→    status: str = "open"
    66→    chronic: bool = False
    67→
    68→    # Subjective gating
    69→    include_subjective: bool = True
    70→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   101→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   102→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   103→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   104→    """
   105→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   106→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   107→
   108→    # 1. Gather
   109→    items = build_issue_items(
   110→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   111→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   112→    )
   113→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   114→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   115→
   116→    # 2. Score & filter
   117→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   118→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   119→
   120→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   121→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   124→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   125→
   126→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   127→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   128→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   129→
   130→    # 6. Finalize
   131→    if not items:
   132→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   133→    total = len(items)
   134→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   135→        items = items[:opts.count]
   136→    if opts.explain:
   137→        for item in items:
   138→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   139→
   140→    return {
   141→        "items": items,
   142→        "total": total,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   144→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   145→    }
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
```

```
150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→
   153→def _resolve_inputs(
   154→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   155→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   156→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   157→    ctx = opts.context
   158→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   159→
   160→    scan_path: str | None = (
   161→        state.get("scan_path")
   162→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   163→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    status = opts.status
   167→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   168→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   172→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   173→        threshold = 100.0
   174→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   175→
   176→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   177→
   178→
   179→def _gather_subjective_items(
   180→    state: StateModel,
   181→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   182→    threshold: float,
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   185→
   186→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   187→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   188→    """
   189→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   190→        return []
   191→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   192→        return []
   193→    if opts.chronic:
   194→        return []
   195→
   196→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   197→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   198→    )
   199→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   200→
   201→
   202→def _gather_workflow_items(
   203→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   204→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   205→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   206→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   210→    for builder in (
   211→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   212→        build_import_scores_item,
   213→        build_communicate_score_item,
   214→    ):
   215→        item = builder(plan, state)
   216→        if item is not None:
   217→            items.append(item)
   218→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   219→    if plan_item is not None:
   220→        items.append(plan_item)
   221→    return items
   222→
   223→
   224→_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   225→
   226→
   227→def _passes_impact_floor(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   228→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   229→    if item.get("kind") != "issue":
   230→        return True
   231→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   232→        return True
   233→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")
   234→    return not impact or impact >= _MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   235→
   236→
   237→def _plan_presort(
   238→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   239→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   240→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   241→
   242→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   243→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   244→    """
   245→    if not plan:
   246→        return set(), []
   247→
   248→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   249→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
```
