   218→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   219→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   220→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   221→    state = runtime.state
   222→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   223→        return
   224→
   225→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   226→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   227→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   228→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   229→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   230→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   231→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   232→
   233→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   234→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(
   235→        kind=kind,
   236→        attestation=attestation,
   237→        note=note,
   238→    ):
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   242→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   243→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   244→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   245→    if not issue_ids:
   246→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   247→        return
   248→
   249→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   250→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   251→        kind=kind,
   252→        state_file=state_file,
   253→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   254→        note=note,
   255→        attestation=attestation,
   256→    )
   257→
   258→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   259→    count = skip_items(
   260→        plan,
   261→        issue_ids,
   262→        kind=kind,
   263→        reason=reason,
   264→        note=note,
   265→        attestation=attestation,
   266→        review_after=review_after,
   267→        scan_count=scan_count,
   268→    )
   269→
   270→    # Log the skip action
   271→    append_log_entry(
   272→        plan,
   273→        "skip",
   274→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   275→        actor="user",
   276→        note=note,
   277→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   278→    )
   279→    if state_data is not None:
   280→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   281→            plan=plan,
   282→            plan_path=plan_file,
   283→            state_data=state_data,
   284→            state_path_value=state_file,
   285→        )
   286→    else:
   287→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   288→
   289→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   290→    if review_after:
   291→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   292→    print_user_message(
   293→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   294→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   295→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   296→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   297→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   298→    )

   301→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   302→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   303→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   304→    state = runtime.state
   305→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   306→        return
   307→
   308→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   309→
   310→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   311→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   312→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   313→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   314→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   315→    if not issue_ids:
   316→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   317→        return
   318→
   319→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   320→    append_log_entry(
   321→        plan, "unskip", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   322→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   323→    )
   324→
   325→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   326→    reopened: list[str] = []
   327→    if need_reopen:
   328→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   329→        for fid in need_reopen:
   330→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   331→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   332→            plan=plan,
   333→            plan_path=plan_file,
   334→            state_data=state_data,
   335→            state_path_value=state_file,
   336→        )
   337→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   338→    else:
   339→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   340→
   341→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   342→

   348→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   349→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   350→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   351→
   352→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   353→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   354→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   355→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   356→
   357→    reopened: list[str] = []
   358→    for pattern in patterns:
   359→        reopened.extend(
   360→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   361→        )
   362→
   363→    if not reopened:
   364→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   365→        return
   366→
   367→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   368→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   369→
   370→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   371→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   372→
   373→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   374→    count = 0
   375→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   376→    for fid in reopened:
   377→        if fid in skipped:
   378→            skipped.pop(fid)
   379→            count += 1
   380→        if fid not in order:
   381→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   382→            order.add(fid)
   383→            count += 1
   384→    append_log_entry(
   385→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   386→    )
   387→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   388→        plan=plan,
   389→        plan_path=plan_file,
   390→        state_data=state_data,
   391→        state_path_value=state_file,
   392→    )
   393→
   394→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   395→    if count:
   396→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))

   399→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   400→
   401→
   402→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   403→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   404→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   405→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   406→    for pattern in patterns:
   407→        if pattern in clusters:
   408→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   409→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   410→            ids = [
   411→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   412→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   413→            ]
   414→            if len(ids) == 0:
   415→                print(colorize(
   416→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   417→                    "yellow",
   418→                ))
   419→                print(colorize(
   420→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   421→                    "dim",
   422→                ))
   423→                return True  # blocked
   424→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   425→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   426→                return True  # blocked
   427→    return False  # OK
   428→
   429→
   430→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   431→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   432→    print(colorize(
   433→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   434→        "yellow",
   435→    ))
   436→    for fid in issue_ids:
   437→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   438→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   439→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   440→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   441→    print(colorize(
   442→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   443→        "dim",
   444→    ))
   445→    print(colorize(
   446→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   447→        "dim",
   448→    ))

   450→
   451→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   452→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   453→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   454→
   455→
   456→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   457→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   458→
   459→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   460→    """
   461→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   462→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   463→    return synthetic, remaining
   464→
   465→
   466→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   467→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   468→
   469→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   470→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   471→    """
   472→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   473→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   474→    if not present:
   475→        return {}
   476→
   477→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   478→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   479→
   480→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   481→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   482→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   483→            continue
   484→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   485→        unmet = sorted(
   486→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   487→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   488→        )
   489→        if unmet:
   490→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   491→    return blocked
   492→
   493→
   494→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   495→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   496→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   497→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   498→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   499→
   500→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   501→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   502→        if not note:
   503→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   504→            return
   505→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   506→        args.attest = attestation
   507→
   508→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   509→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   510→    if synthetic_ids:
   511→        plan = load_plan()
   512→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   513→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   514→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   515→            if sid in blocked_map:
   516→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   517→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   518→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   519→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   520→                    return
   521→
   522→        # Gate workflow items behind triage completion
   523→        GATED_WORKFLOW_IDS = {WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID}
   524→        gated_ids = [sid for sid in synthetic_ids if sid in GATED_WORKFLOW_IDS]
   525→        if gated_ids:
   526→            force = getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)
   527→            meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   528→            # Triage is complete if last_completed_at is set (stages archive on --complete)
   529→            triage_ever_completed = bool(meta.get("last_completed_at"))
   530→            if triage_ever_completed:
   531→                # --complete archives stages and sets last_completed_at → all 4 done
   532→                missing: set[str] = set()
   533→            else:
   534→                confirmed_stages = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   535→                required_stages = {"observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit"}
   536→                missing = required_stages - confirmed_stages
   537→
   538→            if missing and not force:
   539→                # Auto-inject triage stages if not already in queue
   540→                if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   541→                    inject_triage_stages(plan)
   542→                    meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   543→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   544→                    save_plan(plan)
   545→
   546→                # Print targeted error based on which stage is next
   547→                stage_order = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit"]
   548→                next_stage = next((s for s in stage_order if s in missing), "observe")
   549→
   550→                for wid in gated_ids:
   551→                    print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {wid} — triage not complete.", "red"))
   552→                print()
   553→
   554→                if next_stage == "observe":
   555→                    print(colorize("  You must analyze the findings before resolving this.", "yellow"))
   556→                    print(colorize("  Start by examining themes, root causes, and contradictions:", "dim"))
   557→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   558→                    print()
   559→                    print(colorize("  The report must be 100+ chars describing what you found.", "dim"))
   560→                elif next_stage == "reflect":
   561→                    print(colorize("  Observe is done. Now compare against previously completed work:", "yellow"))
   562→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   563→                    print()
   564→                    print(colorize("  The report must mention recurring dimensions if any exist.", "dim"))
   565→                elif next_stage == "organize":
   566→                    print(colorize("  Reflect is done. Now create clusters and prioritize:", "yellow"))
   567→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   568→                    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
   569→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
   570→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."', "dim"))
   571→                    print()
   572→                    print(colorize("  All manual clusters must have descriptions and action_steps.", "dim"))
   573→                elif next_stage == "commit":
   574→                    print(colorize("  Organize is done. Finalize the execution plan:", "yellow"))
   575→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   576→
   577→                print()
   578→                print(colorize(f"  Remaining stages: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}", "dim"))
   579→                print(colorize(
   580→                    "  To skip triage: --force-resolve --note 'reason for skipping triage'",
   581→                    "dim",
   582→                ))
   583→
   584→                # Log the blocked attempt
   585→                append_log_entry(
   586→                    plan, "workflow_blocked",
   587→                    issue_ids=gated_ids, actor="user",
   588→                    note=note,
   589→                    detail={"missing_stages": sorted(missing), "next_stage": next_stage},
   590→                )
   591→                save_plan(plan)
   592→                return
   593→
   594→            if missing and force:
   595→                # Force-resolve: require substantial note, log prominently
   596→                if not note or len(note.strip()) < 50:
   597→                    print(colorize(
   598→                        f"  --force-resolve still requires --note (min 50 chars) explaining "
   599→                        f"why you're skipping triage.",
   600→                        "red",
   601→                    ))
   602→                    return
   603→                print(colorize(
   604→                    "  WARNING: Skipping triage requirement — this is logged.",
   605→                    "yellow",
   606→                ))
   607→                append_log_entry(
   608→                    plan, "workflow_force_skip",
   609→                    issue_ids=gated_ids, actor="user",
   610→                    note=note,
   611→                    detail={"forced": True, "missing_stages": sorted(missing)},
   612→                )
   613→                save_plan(plan)
   614→                # Fall through to scan gate / purge_ids below
   615→
   616→        # Gate workflow items behind scan completion
   617→        if gated_ids:
   618→            scan_count_at_start = plan.get("scan_count_at_plan_start")
   619→            if scan_count_at_start is not None:
   620→                resolved_state_path = state_path(args)
   621→                state_data = state_mod.load_state(resolved_state_path)
   622→                current_scan_count = int(state_data.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   623→                scan_ran = current_scan_count > scan_count_at_start
   624→                scan_skipped = plan.get("scan_gate_skipped", False)
   625→
   626→                if not scan_ran and not scan_skipped and not force:
   627→                    for wid in gated_ids:
   628→                        print(colorize(
   629→                            f"  Cannot resolve {wid} — no scan has run this cycle.",
   630→                            "red",
   631→                        ))
   632→                    print()
   633→                    print(colorize(
   634→                        "  You must run a scan before resolving workflow items:",

   637→                    print(colorize("    desloppify scan", "dim"))
   638→                    print()
   639→                    print(colorize(

   644→                    print(colorize(

   649→                    print(colorize(

   653→
   654→                    append_log_entry(

   662→                    )
   663→                    save_plan(plan)
   664→                    return
   665→
   666→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   667→        append_log_entry(
   668→            plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   669→        )
   670→        save_plan(plan)
   671→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   672→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   673→        if not real_patterns:
   674→            return
   675→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   676→        patterns = real_patterns
   677→        args.patterns = patterns
   678→
   679→    # Validate note length
   680→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   681→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   682→        return
   683→
   684→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   685→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   686→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   687→        return
   688→
   689→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   690→    try:
   691→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   692→        state = runtime.state
   693→        plan = load_plan()
   694→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   695→            return
   696→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   697→        plan = None
   698→
   699→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   700→    try:
   701→        if plan is None:
   702→            plan = load_plan()
   703→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   704→        cluster_name = None
   705→        for p in patterns:
   706→            if p in clusters:
   707→                cluster_name = p
   708→                break
   709→        append_log_entry(
   710→            plan,
   711→            "done",
   712→            issue_ids=patterns,
   713→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   714→            actor="user",
   715→            note=note,
   716→        )
   717→        save_plan(plan)
   718→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   719→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
   720→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   721→
   722→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   723→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   724→        status="fixed",
   725→        patterns=patterns,
   726→        note=note,
   727→        attest=attestation,
   728→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   729→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   730→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),

   732→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   733→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   734→    )
   735→
   736→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)

   739→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   740→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   741→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   742→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   743→
   744→    plan = load_plan()
   745→    if clear_flag:
   746→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   747→        clear_focus(plan)
   748→        append_log_entry(
   749→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   750→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   751→        )
   752→        save_plan(plan)
   753→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   754→        return
   755→
   756→    if not cluster_name:
   757→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   758→        if active:
   759→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   760→        else:
   761→            print("  No active focus.")
   762→        return
   763→
   764→    try:
   765→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   766→    except ValueError as ex:
   767→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   768→        return
   769→    append_log_entry(
   770→        plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   771→        detail={"action": "set"},
   772→    )
   773→    save_plan(plan)
   774→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   775→
   776→
   777→def cmd_plan_scan_gate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   778→    """Check or skip the scan requirement for workflow items."""
   779→    skip = getattr(args, "skip", False)
   780→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   781→
   782→    plan = load_plan()
   783→    scan_count_at_start = plan.get("scan_count_at_plan_start")
   784→
   785→    if scan_count_at_start is None:
   786→        print(colorize("  No active plan cycle (plan_start_scores not seeded).", "dim"))
   787→        print(colorize("  Scan gate is not applicable — workflow items gate themselves.", "dim"))
   788→        return
   789→
   790→    resolved_state_path = state_path(args)
   791→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(resolved_state_path)
   792→    current_scan_count = int(state_data.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   793→    scan_ran = current_scan_count > scan_count_at_start
   794→    scan_skipped = plan.get("scan_gate_skipped", False)
   795→
   796→    if not skip:
   797→        # Status check
   798→        if scan_ran:
   799→            print(colorize("  Scan gate: PASSED", "green"))
   800→            print(colorize(
   801→                f"  Scans at cycle start: {scan_count_at_start}  "
   802→                f"Current: {current_scan_count}",
   803→                "dim",
   804→            ))
   805→        elif scan_skipped:
   806→            print(colorize("  Scan gate: SKIPPED (manually)", "yellow"))
   807→        else:
   808→            print(colorize("  Scan gate: BLOCKED", "red"))
   809→            print(colorize(
   810→                f"  Scans at cycle start: {scan_count_at_start}  "
   811→                f"Current: {current_scan_count}",
   812→                "dim",
   813→            ))
   814→            print(colorize("  Run: desloppify scan", "dim"))
   815→            print(colorize(
   816→                '  Or:  desloppify plan scan-gate --skip --note "reason"',
   817→                "dim",
   818→            ))
   819→        return
   820→
   821→    # --skip mode
   822→    if scan_ran:
   823→        print(colorize("  Scan already ran this cycle — no skip needed.", "green"))
   824→        return
   825→
   826→    if not note or len(note.strip()) < 50:
   827→        print(colorize(
   828→            "  --skip requires --note with at least 50 chars explaining why.",
   829→            "red",
   830→        ))
   831→        return
   832→
   833→    plan["scan_gate_skipped"] = True
   834→    append_log_entry(
   835→        plan, "scan_gate_skip",
   836→        actor="user",
   837→        note=note,
   838→        detail={
   839→            "scan_count_at_start": scan_count_at_start,
   840→            "current_scan_count": current_scan_count,
   841→        },
   842→    )
   843→    save_plan(plan)
   844→    print(colorize("  Scan requirement marked as satisfied (logged).", "yellow"))
   845→
   846→
   847→__all__ = [
   848→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   849→    "cmd_plan_resolve",

   852→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   853→    "cmd_plan_scan_gate",
   854→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   855→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   856→]
   857→

    56→class ClusterContext(NamedTuple):
    57→    cluster_name: str | None
    58→    cluster_completed: bool
    59→    cluster_remaining: int
    60→
    61→
    62→def _capture_cluster_context(
    63→    plan: dict, resolved_ids: list[str],
    64→) -> ClusterContext:
    65→    """Determine cluster membership for resolved issues, pre-purge."""
    66→    clusters = plan.get("clusters") or {}
    67→    overrides = plan.get("overrides") or {}
    68→    # Find the cluster the first resolved issue belongs to
    69→    cluster_name: str | None = None
    70→    for rid in resolved_ids:
    71→        ov = overrides.get(rid)
    72→        if ov and ov.get("cluster"):
    73→            cluster_name = ov["cluster"]
    74→            break
    75→    if not cluster_name or cluster_name not in clusters:
    76→        return ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
    77→    # Count how many will remain after these ids are removed
    78→    current_ids = set(clusters[cluster_name].get("issue_ids") or [])
    79→    remaining = current_ids - set(resolved_ids)
    80→    return ClusterContext(
    81→        cluster_name=cluster_name,
    82→        cluster_completed=len(remaining) == 0,
    83→        cluster_remaining=len(remaining),
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    88→    if args.status != "fixed":
    89→        return True
    90→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    91→    if validate_note_length(note):
    92→        return True
    93→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    94→    return False
    95→

    97→def _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    98→    *,
    99→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   100→    all_resolved: list[str],
   101→    attestation: str | None,
   102→) -> tuple[dict | None, ClusterContext]:
   103→    plan = None
   104→    ctx = ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
   105→    try:
   106→        if not has_living_plan():
   107→            return None, ctx
   108→        plan = load_plan()
   109→        ctx = _capture_cluster_context(plan, all_resolved)
   110→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   111→        append_log_entry(
   112→            plan,
   113→            "resolve",
   114→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
   115→            actor="user",
   116→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
   117→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
   118→        )
   119→        if ctx.cluster_completed and ctx.cluster_name:
   120→            append_log_entry(
   121→                plan,
   122→                "cluster_done",
   123→                issue_ids=all_resolved,
   124→                cluster_name=ctx.cluster_name,
   125→                actor="user",
   126→            )
   127→        # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
   128→        if args.status == "fixed":
   129→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
   130→        elif args.status == "open":
   131→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   132→        save_plan(plan)
   133→        if purged:
   134→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   135→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   136→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
   137→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   138→    return plan, ctx

   141→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
   143→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   144→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
   145→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    state_file = state_path(args)
   149→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   150→
   151→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    if args.status == "fixed":
   155→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
   156→            state=state,
   157→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   158→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
   159→        )
   160→
   161→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   162→        state,
   163→        args,
   164→        attestation=attestation,
   165→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
   166→    )
   167→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   168→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   169→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   170→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   171→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
   175→    if not all_resolved:
   176→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
   177→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   181→
   182→    plan, cluster_ctx = _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   183→        args=args,
   184→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   185→        attestation=attestation,
   186→    )
   187→    mid_cluster = (
   188→        cluster_ctx.cluster_name is not None and not cluster_ctx.cluster_completed
   189→    )
   190→
   191→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   192→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   193→        state,
   194→        status=args.status,
   195→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   196→    )
   197→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   198→    if not mid_cluster:
   199→        render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   200→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   201→        args=args,
   202→        state=state,
   203→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   204→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   208→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   209→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   210→        state,
   211→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   212→    )
   213→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   214→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   215→
   216→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   217→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   218→        ResolveQueryContext(
   219→            patterns=args.patterns,
   220→            status=args.status,
   221→            resolved=all_resolved,
   222→            next_command=next_command,
   223→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   224→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   225→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   226→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   227→            attestation=attestation,
   228→            narrative=narrative,
   229→            state=state,
   230→        )
   231→    )
   232→
   233→    if args.status == "fixed" and plan is not None and next_command == "desloppify next":
   234→        if mid_cluster:
   235→            print_user_message(
   236→                f"Hey — task done, {cluster_ctx.cluster_remaining} left in"
   237→                f" cluster '{cluster_ctx.cluster_name}'. Run `desloppify next`"
   238→                " to pick up the next one. Keep going — don't stop until the"
   239→                " cluster is finished."
   240→            )
   241→        elif cluster_ctx.cluster_completed and cluster_ctx.cluster_name:
   242→            print_user_message(
   243→                f"Hey — nice, you just finished cluster '{cluster_ctx.cluster_name}'."
   244→                " Check the commit tracking above — this is a good time to"
   245→                " commit and push your work. Then run `desloppify next` to"
   246→                " pick up your next task."
   247→            )
   248→        else:
   249→            print_user_message(
   250→                "Hey — nice, on to the next one. Run `desloppify next`"
   251→                " to pick up your next task. Before moving on, glance at the"
   252→                " commit tracking above — if there's a decent chunk of"
   253→                " uncommitted work, consider whether now's a good time"
   254→                " to commit and push. Otherwise just keep going."
   255→            )
   256→
   257→
   258→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_resolve"]
   259→

1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    16→
    17→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _front_item_ids(front_item: dict) -> tuple[str, set[str]]:
    21→    front_id = front_item["id"]
    22→    front_ids = {front_id}
    23→    if front_item.get("kind") != "cluster":
    24→        return front_id, front_ids
    25→    for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    26→        front_ids.add(member["id"])
    27→    return front_id, front_ids
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_target_ids(patterns: list[str], clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    31→    resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    32→    for pattern in patterns:
    33→        if pattern in clusters:
    34→            resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("issue_ids", []))
    35→            resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    36→            continue
    37→        resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    38→    return resolved_ids
    39→
    40→
    41→def _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
    42→    resolved_ids: set[str],
    43→    *,
    44→    clusters: dict,
    45→    issues: dict,
    46→) -> set[str]:
    47→    return {
    48→        issue_id
    49→        for issue_id in resolved_ids
    50→        if issue_id in clusters
    51→        or (issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open")
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def _prune_front_covered_clusters(
    56→    out_of_order: set[str],
    57→    *,
    58→    clusters: dict,
    59→    issues: dict,
    60→    front_ids: set[str],
    61→) -> None:
    62→    for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    63→        if cluster_id not in clusters:
    64→            continue
    65→        alive_members = {
    66→            issue_id
    67→            for issue_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("issue_ids", [])
    68→            if issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open"
    69→        }
    70→        if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    71→            out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _print_queue_order_violation(front_id: str, out_of_order: set[str]) -> None:
    75→    print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    76→    for issue_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    77→        print(f"    {issue_id}")
    78→    print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    79→    print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan reorder <pattern> top            # move to front", "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    82→    print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    83→
    84→
    85→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    86→    state: dict,
    87→    patterns: list[str],
    88→    status: str,
    89→) -> bool:
    90→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    91→    if status != "fixed":
    92→        return False
    93→    try:
    94→        if not has_living_plan():
    95→            return False
    96→        plan = load_plan()
    97→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    98→        if not queue_order:
    99→            return False
   100→
   101→        ctx = queue_context(state, plan=plan)
   102→        result = build_work_queue(
   103→            state,
   104→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   105→                count=None,
   106→                include_subjective=True,
   107→                context=ctx,
   108→            ),
   109→        )
   110→        items = result["items"]
   111→        if not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   112→            items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   113→        if not items:
   114→            return False
   115→
   116→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   117→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→        resolved_ids = _resolve_target_ids(patterns, clusters)
   119→        resolved_ids = _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
   120→            resolved_ids,
   121→            clusters=clusters,
   122→            issues=issues,
   123→        )
   124→        if not resolved_ids:
   125→            return False
   126→
   127→        # Collect IDs from the first N queue positions where N is the
   128→        # number of items being resolved.  This allows batch-resolving
   129→        # a contiguous prefix (e.g. the first 9 items in one command).
   130→        prefix_depth = max(len(resolved_ids), 1)
   131→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
   132→        front_id = items[0]["id"]
   133→        for item in items[:prefix_depth]:
   134→            _, item_ids = _front_item_ids(item)
   135→            front_ids.update(item_ids)
   136→
   137→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
   138→        _prune_front_covered_clusters(
   139→            out_of_order,
   140→            clusters=clusters,
   141→            issues=issues,
   142→            front_ids=front_ids,
   143→        )
   144→        if not out_of_order:
   145→            return False
   146→
   147→        _print_queue_order_violation(front_id, out_of_order)
   148→        return True
   149→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   150→        _logger.debug("queue order guard skipped", exc_info=True)
   151→        return False
   152→
   153→
   154→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
   155→

    48→class NextOptions:
    49→    """All user-facing options for the ``next`` command, extracted once."""
    50→

    63→        """Build from an argparse Namespace, applying defaults for missing attrs."""
    64→        return cls(

    79→    dim_scores: dict,
    80→) -> list[dict]:
    81→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    82→    return _scorecard_subjective_impl(state, dim_scores)

    84→
    85→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    86→    state: dict,
    87→    dim_scores: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    90→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    91→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    92→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    93→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    94→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    95→            continue
    96→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    97→        if strict_val < threshold:
    98→            low.append(
    99→                (
   100→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
   101→                    strict_val,
   102→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   103→                )
   104→            )
   105→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
   106→    return low

   108→
   109→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   110→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
   111→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   112→    state = runtime.state
   113→    config = runtime.config
   114→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   115→        return
   116→
   117→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   118→    if skill_warning:
   119→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   120→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   121→    if config_warning:
   122→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   123→
   124→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   125→    _build_and_render_queue(args, state, config)
   126→
   127→
   128→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
   129→    plan_data: dict | None,
   130→    *,
   131→    cluster_arg: str | None,
   132→    scope: str | None,
   133→) -> str | None:
   134→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   135→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   136→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   137→        if active_cluster:
   138→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   139→    return effective_cluster
   140→
   141→
   142→def _build_next_payload(
   143→    *,
   144→    queue: dict,
   145→    items: list[dict],
   146→    state: dict,
   147→    narrative: dict,
   148→    plan_data: dict | None,
   149→) -> dict:
   150→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   151→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   152→    )
   153→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   154→    payload["overall_score"] = scores.overall
   155→    payload["objective_score"] = scores.objective
   156→    payload["strict_score"] = scores.strict
   157→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   158→        state,
   159→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   160→    )
   161→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   162→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   163→    ]
   164→    return payload
   165→
   166→
   167→def _emit_requested_output(
   168→    opts: NextOptions,
   169→    payload: dict,
   170→    items: list[dict],
   171→) -> bool:
   172→    if opts.output_file:
   173→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   174→            opts.output_file,
   175→            payload,
   176→            len(items),
   177→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   178→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   179→        ):
   180→            return True
   181→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   182→
   183→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(opts.output_format, payload, items):
   184→        return True
   185→    return False
   186→
   187→
   188→def _plan_queue_context(
   189→    *,
   190→    state: dict,
   191→    plan_data: dict | None,
   192→    context=None,
   193→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   194→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan_data
   195→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(effective_plan)
   196→    try:
   197→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data, context=context)
   198→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   199→        breakdown = None
   200→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   201→
   202→
   203→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   204→    try:
   205→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   206→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   207→        return raw_potentials or None
   208→
   209→
   210→def _build_and_render_queue(args: argparse.Namespace, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   211→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   212→
   213→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   214→
   215→    # Load the living plan
   216→    plan = load_plan()
   217→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   218→    if (
   219→        plan.get("queue_order")
   220→        or plan.get("overrides")
   221→        or plan.get("clusters")
   222→    ):
   223→        plan_data = plan
   224→
   225→    # Build unified context once — all downstream consumers agree on
   226→    # plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
   227→    ctx = queue_context(
   228→        state, config=config, plan=plan_data, target_strict=target_strict,
   229→    )
   230→
   231→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   232→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   233→        plan_data,
   234→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   235→        scope=opts.scope,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    queue = build_work_queue(
   239→        state,
   240→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   241→            count=None,
   242→            scope=opts.scope,
   243→            status=opts.status,
   244→            include_subjective=True,
   245→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   246→            explain=opts.explain,
   247→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   248→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   249→            context=ctx,
   250→        ),
   251→    )
   252→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   253→
   254→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   255→    if plan_data and not effective_cluster and not plan_data.get("active_cluster"):
   256→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan_data)
   257→
   258→    # Apply count truncation after collapsing
   259→    if opts.count:
   260→        items = items[: opts.count]
   261→        queue["items"] = items
   262→        queue["total"] = len(items)
   263→
   264→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   265→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   266→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   267→        state,
   268→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   272→        queue=queue,
   273→        items=items,
   274→        state=state,
   275→        narrative=narrative,
   276→        plan_data=plan_data,
   277→    )
   278→    write_query(payload)
   279→
   280→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items):
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   284→    issues_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   285→        state.get("issues", {}),
   286→        state.get("scan_path"),
   287→    )
   288→
   289→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   290→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   291→        state=state,
   292→        plan_data=plan_data,
   293→        context=ctx,
   294→    )
   295→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   296→
   297→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   298→    strict_score = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   299→    if _show_empty_queue(
   300→        queue,
   301→        strict_score,
   302→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   303→        target_strict=target_strict,
   304→    ):
   305→        return
   306→
   307→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   308→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   309→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   310→        items, dim_scores, issues_scoped, group=opts.group, explain=opts.explain,
   311→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   312→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   313→    )
   314→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   315→    next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   316→    next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   317→        state,
   318→        dim_scores,
   319→        issues_scoped,
   320→        strict_score=strict_score,
   321→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   322→        queue_total=queue_total,
   323→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   324→        breakdown=breakdown,
   325→    )
   326→    print()
   327→
   328→    if items and plan_data:
   329→        print_user_message(
   330→            "Hey — the task details are printed above. Just start"
   331→            " working on it. When you're done, resolve it with"
   332→            " `desloppify plan resolve` and then run `desloppify"
   333→            " next` again. If this doesn't seem like the right"
   334→            " priority, run `desloppify plan show` to see the full"
   335→            " queue, or `desloppify plan --help` for all plan tools."
   336→            " Don't stop until you're done — unless I've told you"
   337→            " otherwise, just keep going."
   338→        )

    53→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:

    55→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    56→        return "[review]"
    57→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    58→        return "[auto]"
    59→    return ""
    60→
    61→
    62→def render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    63→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    64→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    65→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    66→        for item in grouped_items:
    67→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    68→            tag = effort_tag(item)
    69→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    70→            print(
    71→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    72→            )
    73→
    74→
    75→def _cluster_type_label(cluster_name: str, action_type: str) -> str:
    76→    if cluster_name in _CLUSTER_NAME_LABELS:
    77→        return _CLUSTER_NAME_LABELS[cluster_name]
    78→    return _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    79→
    80→
    81→def _render_cluster_files(members: list[dict]) -> None:
    82→    file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
    83→    if len(file_counts) <= 5:
    84→        print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
    85→        for filename, count in file_counts.most_common():
    86→            print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
    87→        return
    88→
    89→    print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
    90→    for filename, count in file_counts.most_common(3):
    91→        print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
    92→    remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
    93→    print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def _render_cluster_sample(members: list[dict]) -> None:
    97→    print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
    98→    for member in members[:3]:
    99→        print(f"    - {member.get('id', '')}")
   100→    if len(members) > 3:
   101→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   102→
   103→
   104→def _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   105→    print(colorize(f"\n  Skip:          desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   106→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   107→    print(
   108→        colorize(
   109→            f'  Resolve all:   desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   110→            "dim",
   111→        )
   112→    )
   113→
   114→
   115→def _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   116→    print(
   117→        colorize(
   118→            f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   119→            "dim",
   120→        )
   121→    )
   122→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   123→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   124→
   125→
   126→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   127→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   128→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   129→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   130→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   131→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   132→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(cluster_name, action_type)
   133→    optional_tag = " — optional" if is_optional else ""
   134→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{optional_tag})", "bold"))
   135→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   136→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   137→
   138→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   139→    if action_steps:
   140→        print(colorize(
   141→            f"  [plan: {len(action_steps)} steps] drill in to view",
   142→            "dim",
   143→        ))
   144→
   145→    members = item.get("members", [])
   146→    if members:
   147→        _render_cluster_files(members)
   148→        _render_cluster_sample(members)
   149→
   150→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   151→    if primary_command:
   152→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   153→
   154→    if is_optional:
   155→        _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   159→
   160→
   161→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   162→    del explain
   163→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   164→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   165→    # When the only item is the "run scan" workflow action, show "Queue cleared"
   166→    # instead of a misleading "1 items" count.
   167→    if (
   168→        total == 1
   169→        and len(items) == 1
   170→        and items[0].get("id") == "workflow::run-scan"
   171→    ):
   172→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared (1 workflow step)", "bold"))
   173→    else:
   174→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   175→
   176→
   177→def show_empty_queue(
   178→    queue: dict,
   179→    strict: float | None,
   180→    *,
   181→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   182→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   183→) -> bool:
   184→    del target_strict
   185→    if queue.get("items"):
   186→        return False
   187→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   188→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   189→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   190→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   191→        print(colorize(
   192→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   193→            "cyan",
   194→        ))
   195→        print(colorize(
   196→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   197→            "dim",
   198→        ))
   199→        return True
   200→
   201→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   202→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   203→    return True
   204→
   205→
   206→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   207→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   208→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   209→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   210→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   211→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   212→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) and (plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   213→        tag_str += " [plan]"
   214→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   215→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   216→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   217→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   218→
   219→
   220→__all__ = [
   221→    "effort_tag",
   222→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   223→    "render_cluster_item",
   224→    "render_compact_item",
   225→    "render_grouped",
   226→    "render_queue_header",
   227→    "scorecard_subjective",
   228→    "show_empty_queue",
   229→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   230→]

1→"""Pure grouping/description/action strategy for plan auto-clustering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DetectorMeta
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import AUTO_PREFIX
     9→
    10→
    11→def extract_subtype(issue: dict) -> str | None:
    12→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a issue."""
    13→    detail = issue.get("detail") or {}
    14→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    15→    if kind:
    16→        return kind
    17→
    18→    issue_id = issue.get("id", "")
    19→    parts = issue_id.split("::")
    20→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    21→        candidate = parts[-1]
    22→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    23→            return candidate
    24→    return None
    25→
    26→
    27→def grouping_key(issue: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    28→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a issue."""
    29→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    30→
    31→    if meta is None:
    32→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    33→
    34→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    35→        detail = issue.get("detail") or {}
    36→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    37→        if dimension:
    38→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    39→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    40→
    41→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in ("structural", "responsibility_cohesion"):
    42→        file_path = issue.get("file", "")
    43→        if file_path:
    44→            basename = os.path.basename(file_path)
    45→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    46→
    47→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    48→        subtype = extract_subtype(issue)
    49→        if subtype:
    50→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    51→
    52→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    53→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    54→
    55→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    56→
    57→
    58→def cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    59→    """Convert a grouping key to an ``auto/...`` cluster name."""
    60→    parts = key.split("::")
    61→    if len(parts) == 2:
    62→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    63→        if prefix_type == "review":
    64→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
    65→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
    66→    if len(parts) == 3:
    67→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
    68→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
    69→
    70→
    71→def generate_description(
    72→    cluster_name: str,
    73→    members: list[dict],
    74→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
    75→    subtype: str | None,
    76→) -> str:
    77→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
    78→    _ = cluster_name
    79→    count = len(members)
    80→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
    81→
    82→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    83→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
    84→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
    85→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review issues"
    86→
    87→    if detector == "structural":
    88→        files = {os.path.basename(member.get("file", "")) for member in members}
    89→        if len(files) == 1:
    90→            return f"Review file size: {next(iter(files))}"
    91→        return f"Review {count} large files"
    92→
    93→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
    94→    if subtype:
    95→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
    96→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
    97→
    98→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    99→        return f"Remove {count} {display} issues"
   100→
   101→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   102→
   103→
   104→def subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   105→    """Check if a subtype maps to a detector fixer."""
   106→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   107→        return None
   108→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   109→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   110→        return fixer_name
   111→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   112→        if subtype in fixer:
   113→            return fixer
   114→    return None
   115→
   116→
   117→def strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   118→    """Strip subtype examples from guidance text."""
   119→    if " — " in guidance:
   120→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   121→    return guidance
   122→
   123→
   124→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   125→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   126→    "refactor": "review and refactor each issue",
   127→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each issue",
   128→}
   129→
   130→
   131→def generate_action(
   132→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   133→    subtype: str | None,
   134→) -> str:
   135→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata."""
   136→    if meta is None:
   137→        return "review and fix each issue"
   138→
   139→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   140→        matched_fixer = subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   141→        if matched_fixer:
   142→            return f"desloppify autofix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   143→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   144→        return f"desloppify autofix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   145→
   146→    if meta.tool == "move":
   147→        return "desloppify move"
   148→
   149→    if meta.guidance:
   150→        if subtype:
   151→            return strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   152→        return meta.guidance
   153→
   154→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(meta.action_type, "review and fix each issue")
   155→
   156→
   157→__all__ = [
   158→    "cluster_name_from_key",
   159→    "generate_action",
   160→    "generate_description",
   161→    "grouping_key",
   162→    "strip_guidance_examples",
   163→]
   164→
```

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 25
    12→
    13→
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "kind": member.get("kind", "issue"),
    19→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": member.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    23→        "status": member.get("status"),
    24→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    29→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    30→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    31→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    32→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    33→        serialized_members = [
    34→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    35→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    36→        ]
    37→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    38→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    39→            "id": item.get("id"),
    40→            "kind": "cluster",
    41→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    42→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    43→            "member_count": member_count,
    44→            "members": serialized_members,
    45→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    46→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    47→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    48→        }
    49→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    51→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    52→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    53→        action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
    54→        if action_steps:
    55→            serialized_cluster["action_steps"] = action_steps
    56→        return serialized_cluster
    57→
    58→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    59→        "id": item.get("id"),
    60→        "kind": item.get("kind", "issue"),
    61→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    62→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    63→        "file": item.get("file"),
    64→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    65→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    66→        "status": item.get("status"),
    67→    }
    68→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    69→
    70→    # Workflow dependency state
    71→    if item.get("blocked_by"):
    72→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    73→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    74→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    75→
    76→    explain = item.get("explain")
    77→    if explain is not None:
    78→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    79→
    80→    # Plan metadata
    81→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    82→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    83→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    84→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    85→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    86→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    87→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    88→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    89→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    90→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    91→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    92→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    93→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    94→
    95→    return serialized
    96→
    97→
    98→def build_query_payload(
    99→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
   100→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   101→    *,
   102→    command: str,
   103→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   104→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   105→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   106→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   107→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   108→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   109→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   110→    }
   111→
   112→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   113→        "command": command,
   114→        "items": serialized,
   115→        "queue": queue_section,
   116→        "narrative": narrative,
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    if plan and (
   120→        plan.get("queue_order")
   121→        or plan.get("skipped")
   122→        or plan.get("clusters")
   123→    ):
   124→        clusters_summary = []
   125→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   126→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   127→            clusters_summary.append({
   128→                "name": name,
   129→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   130→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   131→            })
   132→        payload["plan"] = {
   133→            "active": True,
   134→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   135→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   136→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   137→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   138→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   139→        }
   140→
   141→    return payload
   142→
   143→
   144→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   145→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   146→    lines = [
   147→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   148→        "",
   149→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   150→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   151→    ]
   152→    for item in items:
   153→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   154→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   155→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   156→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   157→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   158→    lines.append("")
   159→    return "\n".join(lines)
   160→
   161→
   162→def write_output_file(
   163→    output_file: str,
   164→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   165→    item_count: int,
   166→    *,
   167→    safe_write_text_fn,
   168→    colorize_fn,
   169→) -> bool:
   170→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   171→    try:
   172→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   173→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   174→    except OSError as exc:
   175→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   176→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   177→        return False
   178→    return True
   179→
   180→
   181→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   182→    output_format: str,
   183→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   184→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   185→) -> bool:
   186→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   187→    renderers = {
   188→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   189→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   190→    }
   191→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   192→    if renderer is None:
   193→        return False
   194→    renderer()
   195→    return True
   196→
   197→
   198→__all__ = [
   199→    "build_query_payload",
   200→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   201→    "render_markdown",
   202→    "serialize_item",
   203→    "write_output_file",
   204→]
   205→
```

     3→from __future__ import annotations

    22→def skip_kind_from_flags(*, permanent: bool, false_positive: bool) -> str:

1→"""Shared attestation and note validation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    10→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    11→_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH = 50
    12→
    13→
    14→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    15→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    16→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    20→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    21→    return [
    22→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    23→    ]
    24→
    25→
    26→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    27→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    28→
    29→
    30→def show_attestation_requirement(
    31→    label: str,
    32→    attestation: str | None,
    33→    example: str,
    34→) -> None:
    35→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    36→    if not attestation:
    37→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    38→    elif missing:
    39→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    40→        _emit_warning(
    41→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    42→        )
    43→    _emit_warning(
    44→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    45→    )
    46→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    47→
    48→
    49→def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:
    50→    """Return True if the note meets the minimum length requirement."""
    51→    return note is not None and len(note.strip()) >= _MIN_NOTE_LENGTH
    52→
    53→
    54→def show_note_length_requirement(note: str | None) -> None:
    55→    """Emit a warning about minimum note length."""
    56→    current = len((note or "").strip())
    57→    _emit_warning(
    58→        f"Note must be at least {_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH} characters (got {current}). "
    59→        f"Describe what you actually did."
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = [
    64→    "_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH",
    65→    "show_attestation_requirement",
    66→    "show_note_length_requirement",
    67→    "validate_attestation",
    68→    "validate_note_length",
    69→]
    70→

    10→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    13→
    14→ALL_STATUSES = set(issue_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    15→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    16→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (

    22→def detail_dict(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    23→    """Return issue detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_issue(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    35→        return True
    36→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    37→        return True
    38→    return False
    39→
