```
1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.attestation import (
    12→    show_attestation_requirement,
    13→    validate_attestation,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    18→
    19→
    20→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    21→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    22→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class ResolveQueryContext:
    27→    patterns: list[str]
    28→    status: str
    29→    resolved: list[str]
    30→    next_command: str
    31→    prev_overall: float | None
    32→    prev_objective: float | None
    33→    prev_strict: float | None
    34→    prev_verified: float | None
    35→    attestation: str | None
    36→    narrative: dict
    37→    state: dict
    38→
    39→
    40→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    41→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    42→        raise CommandError(
    43→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    44→        )
    45→    if args.status == "open":
    46→        return
    47→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    48→        show_attestation_requirement(
    49→            "Manual resolve",
    50→            attestation,
    51→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    52→        )
    53→        raise CommandError("Manual resolve requires a valid attestation.")
    54→
    55→
    56→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
    57→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
    58→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
    62→    """Count unique open issues matching the provided patterns."""
    63→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
    64→    for pattern in patterns:
    65→        for issue in state_mod.match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
    66→            issue_id = issue.get("id")
    67→            if issue_id:
    68→                matched_ids.add(issue_id)
    69→    return len(matched_ids)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
    73→    state: dict,
    74→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    75→    *,
    76→    attestation: str | None,
    77→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
    78→) -> float:
    79→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
    80→    before = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
    81→    if before is None:
    82→        return 0.0
    83→
    84→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
    85→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
    86→    after = state_mod.score_snapshot(preview_state).strict
    87→    if after is None:
    88→        return 0.0
    89→    return max(0.0, before - after)
    90→
    91→
    92→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    93→    state: dict,
    94→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    95→    *,
    96→    attestation: str | None,
    97→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
    98→) -> None:
    99→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   103→    if preview_count <= 10:
   104→        return
   105→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   109→        state,
   110→        args,
   111→        attestation=attestation,
   112→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   113→    )
   114→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} issues).")
   115→    if strict_delta > 0:
   116→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   117→    raise CommandError(
   118→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   119→    )
   120→
```

```
1→"""Internal sync helpers for auto-cluster regeneration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.cluster_strategy import (
     9→    cluster_name_from_key as _cluster_name_from_key,
    10→    generate_action as _generate_action,
    11→    generate_description as _generate_description,
    12→    grouping_key as _grouping_key,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    15→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    16→    _current_stale_ids,
    17→    current_under_target_ids,
    18→    current_unscored_ids,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    21→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    22→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    25→
    26→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    27→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    28→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    30→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    31→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    32→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    33→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    34→
    35→
    36→def _manual_member_ids(clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    37→    """Collect all issue IDs belonging to manual (non-auto) clusters."""
    38→    ids: set[str] = set()
    39→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    40→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    41→            ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    42→    return ids
    43→
    44→
    45→def _group_clusterable_issues(
    46→    issues: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    manual_member_ids: set[str],
    49→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, dict]]:
    50→    """Group open, non-suppressed, non-manual issues by detector/subtype key.
    51→
    52→    Returns (groups_by_key, issue_data) where groups_by_key maps grouping keys
    53→    to lists of issue IDs, filtered to clusters >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE.
    54→    """
    55→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    56→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    57→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
    58→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    59→            continue
    60→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    61→            continue
    62→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    66→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    67→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
    68→        if key is None:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        groups[key].append(fid)
    72→        issue_data[fid] = issue
    73→
    74→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
    75→    return groups, issue_data
    76→
    77→
    78→def _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
    79→    plan: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    clusters: dict,
    82→    existing_name: str,
    83→    member_ids: list[str],
    84→    now: str,
    85→) -> int:
    86→    """Sync member IDs for a user-modified cluster, returns count of changes."""
    87→    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    88→    changes = 0
    89→    existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    90→    new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
    91→    if new_ids:
    92→        cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
    93→        cluster["updated_at"] = now
    94→        changes = 1
    95→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    96→    for fid in member_ids:
    97→        if fid not in overrides:
    98→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    99→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   100→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   101→    return changes
   102→
   103→
   104→def _subjective_state_sets(
   105→    state: StateModel,
   106→    *,
   107→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   108→    target_strict: float,
   109→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
   110→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""
   111→    if policy is not None:
   112→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
   113→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
   114→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
   115→    else:
   116→        unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   117→        stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   118→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   119→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids
   120→
   121→
   122→def _has_objective_backlog(
   123→    issues: dict,
   124→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   125→) -> bool:
   126→    """Check if objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   127→    if policy is not None:
   128→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   129→    return any(
   130→        f.get("status") == "open"
   131→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   132→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   133→        for f in issues.values()
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   138→    plan: dict,
   139→    clusters: dict,
   140→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   141→    *,
   142→    cluster_key: str,
   143→    cluster_name: str,
   144→    member_ids: list[str],
   145→    description: str,
   146→    action: str,
   147→    now: str,
   148→    optional: bool = False,
   149→) -> int:
   150→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries."""
   151→    changes = 0
   152→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   153→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   154→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   155→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   156→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   157→        if (
   158→            old_ids != new_ids_set
   159→            or cluster.get("description") != description
   160→            or cluster.get("action") != action
   161→        ):
   162→            cluster["issue_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   163→            cluster["description"] = description
   164→            cluster["action"] = action
   165→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   166→            changes = 1
   167→    else:
   168→        new_cluster = {
   169→            "name": cluster_name,
   170→            "description": description,
   171→            "issue_ids": list(member_ids),
   172→            "created_at": now,
   173→            "updated_at": now,
   174→            "auto": True,
   175→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   176→            "action": action,
   177→            "user_modified": False,
   178→        }
   179→        if optional:
   180→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   181→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   182→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   183→        changes = 1
   184→
   185→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   186→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   187→    for fid in member_ids:
   188→        if fid not in overrides:
   189→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   190→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   191→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   192→
   193→    return changes
   194→
   195→
   196→def sync_issue_clusters(
   197→    plan: dict,
   198→    issues: dict,
   199→    clusters: dict,
   200→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   201→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   202→    now: str,
   203→) -> int:
   204→    """Group open issues by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   205→    changes = 0
   206→
   207→    groups, issue_data = _group_clusterable_issues(
   208→        issues, manual_member_ids=_manual_member_ids(clusters)
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   212→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   213→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   214→
   215→        rep = issue_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   216→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   217→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   218→        members = [issue_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in issue_data]
   219→
   220→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   221→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   222→
   223→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   224→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   225→
   226→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   227→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   228→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   229→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   230→                changes += _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
   231→                    plan,
   232→                    clusters=clusters,
   233→                    existing_name=existing_name,
   234→                    member_ids=member_ids,
   235→                    now=now,
   236→                )
   237→                continue
   238→
   239→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   240→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   241→
   242→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   243→            plan,
   244→            clusters,
   245→            existing_by_key,
   246→            cluster_key=key,
   247→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   248→            member_ids=member_ids,
   249→            description=description,
   250→            action=action,
   251→            now=now,
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    return changes
   255→
   256→
   257→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   258→    plan: dict,
   259→    state: StateModel,
   260→    issues: dict,
   261→    clusters: dict,
   262→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   263→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   264→    now: str,
   265→    *,
   266→    target_strict: float,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> int:
   270→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   271→    changes = 0
   272→
   273→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   274→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   275→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   279→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   280→    )
   281→
   282→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   283→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   284→    )
   285→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   286→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   287→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   288→    )
   289→
   290→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   291→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   292→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   293→        description = (
   294→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   295→        )
   296→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   297→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   298→            plan,
   299→            clusters,
   300→            existing_by_key,
   301→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   302→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   303→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   304→            description=description,
   305→            action=action,
   306→            now=now,
   307→        )
   308→
   309→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   310→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   311→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   312→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   313→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   314→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   315→            plan,
   316→            clusters,
   317→            existing_by_key,
   318→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   319→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   320→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   321→            description=description,
   322→            action=action,
   323→            now=now,
   324→        )
   325→
   326→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   327→
   328→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   329→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   330→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   331→    ut_prune = [
   332→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   333→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   334→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   335→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   336→        and fid in order
   337→    ]
   338→    for fid in ut_prune:
   339→        order.remove(fid)
   340→        changes += 1
   341→
   342→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   343→
   344→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   345→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   346→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   347→        description = (
   348→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   349→            f"dimensions under target score"
   350→        )
   351→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   352→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   353→            plan,
   354→            clusters,
   355→            existing_by_key,
   356→            [REDACTED],
   357→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   358→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   359→            description=description,
   360→            action=action,
   361→            now=now,
   362→            optional=True,
   363→        )
   364→
   365→        existing_order = set(order)
   366→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   367→            if fid not in existing_order:
   368→                order.append(fid)
   369→
   370→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   371→        objective_evict = [
   372→            fid for fid in order
   373→            if fid in under_target_ids
   374→        ]
   375→        for fid in objective_evict:
   376→            order.remove(fid)
   377→            changes += 1
   378→
   379→    return changes
   380→
   381→
   382→def prune_stale_clusters(
   383→    plan: dict,
   384→    issues: dict,
   385→    clusters: dict,
   386→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   387→    now: str,
   388→) -> int:
   389→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   390→    changes = 0
   391→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   392→        cluster = clusters[name]
   393→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   394→            continue
   395→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   396→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   397→            continue
   398→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   399→            alive = [
   400→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   401→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   402→            ]
   403→            if alive:
   404→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])):
   405→                    cluster["issue_ids"] = alive
   406→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   407→                    changes += 1
   408→                continue
   409→        del clusters[name]
   410→        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   411→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   412→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   413→                override["cluster"] = None
   414→                override["updated_at"] = now
   415→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   416→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   417→        changes += 1
   418→    return changes
   419→
   420→
   421→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   422→
```

```
1→"""Skip/unskip mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_queue import _remove_id_from_lists
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import skip_kind_needs_state_reopen
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    10→
    11→
    12→def skip_items(
    13→    plan: PlanModel,
    14→    issue_ids: list[str],
    15→    *,
    16→    kind: str = "temporary",
    17→    reason: str | None = None,
    18→    note: str | None = None,
    19→    attestation: str | None = None,
    20→    review_after: int | None = None,
    21→    scan_count: int = 0,
    22→) -> int:
    23→    """Move issue IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
    24→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    25→    now = utc_now()
    26→    count = 0
    27→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    28→    skip_set = set(issue_ids)
    29→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, skip_set)
    30→    for fid in issue_ids:
    31→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
    32→        skipped[fid] = {
    33→            "issue_id": fid,
    34→            "kind": kind,
    35→            "reason": reason,
    36→            "note": note,
    37→            "attestation": attestation,
    38→            "created_at": now,
    39→            "review_after": review_after,
    40→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
    41→        }
    42→        count += 1
    43→    return count
    44→
    45→
    46→def unskip_items(
    47→    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]
    48→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    49→    """Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
    50→
    51→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
    52→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
    53→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
    54→    """
    55→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    56→    count = 0
    57→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
    58→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    59→    for fid in issue_ids:
    60→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
    61→        if entry is not None:
    62→            if skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(str(entry.get("kind", ""))):
    63→                need_reopen.append(fid)
    64→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
    65→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
    66→            count += 1
    67→    return count, need_reopen
    68→
    69→
    70→def resurface_stale_skips(
    71→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
    72→) -> list[str]:
    73→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
    74→
    75→    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.
    76→    """
    77→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    78→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    79→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
    80→    for fid in list(skipped):
    81→        entry = skipped[fid]
    82→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
    83→            continue
    84→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
    85→        if review_after is None:
    86→            continue
    87→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
    88→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
    89→            skipped.pop(fid)
    90→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
    91→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
    92→            resurfaced.append(fid)
    93→    return resurfaced
    94→
    95→
    96→__all__ = ["resurface_stale_skips", "skip_items", "unskip_items"]
    97→
```

---

## Desloppify CLI Patterns: Comprehensive Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here are the detailed patterns across the areas you requested:

### 1. CONFIRMATION & GUARD PATTERNS

#### A. **Attestation Requirements** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/attestation.py`)

The CLI uses **dual-keyword attestation validation** for high-risk operations:
- **Required phrases**: `"i have actually"` (lowercase match) AND `"not gaming"` (present anywhere)
- **Example template** (from `ATTEST_EXAMPLE`):
  ```
  I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing.
  ```
- **Applies to**:
  - **Permanent skip** (`--permanent`): Requires attestation + note (min 50 chars)
  - **False positive** (`--false_positive`): Requires attestation (no note requirement)
  - **Fixed resolve** (default status): Requires attestation
  - **Wontfix resolve** (`--status wontfix`): Requires note (min 50 chars), not attestation

#### B. **Skip Operation Guards** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`)

**Three skip kinds** with different validation:
1. **Temporary** (`skip_kind="temporary"`, default): 
   - No attestation required
   - Optional: `--reason` (stored in plan), `--review-after N` (resurface after N scans)
   - No note required
   
2. **Permanent** (`--permanent`):
   - **Requires**: `--attest "..."` (dual-keyword attestation)
   - **Requires**: `--note "..."` (min 50 chars, explains decision)
   - Stored as `status: "wontfix"` in state
   - Attestation logged in plan
   
3. **False Positive** (`--false_positive`):
   - **Requires**: `--attest "..."` (dual-keyword attestation)
   - No note required
   - Stored as `status: "false_positive"` in state

**Validation flow** (lines 171-192):
```python
def _validate_skip_requirements(kind, attestation, note):
    # 1. Check attestation if required by skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind)
    # 2. Check note length if required by skip_kind_requires_note(kind)
    # Return False blocks operation
```

#### C. **Cluster Completion Guard** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`, lines 399-448)

**Guard threshold**: 10 items or fewer in a cluster
- If pattern matches a cluster AND cluster has ≤10 alive members AND cluster is not empty:
  - **Block resolve** with detailed error message
  - Print each member with detector + summary
  - Suggest: resolve individually or drill in first
- Logic: `_check_cluster_guard()` builds queue, checks cluster membership

#### D. **Queue Order Guard** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/queue_guard.py`)

**Prevents out-of-order resolution** when a plan exists:
1. Build actual queue from state
2. Resolve patterns specify which IDs to fix
3. Allow batch-resolve only if all IDs are in the first N positions (N = number of IDs being resolved)
4. Block with helpful message: "items not next in plan queue"
5. Suggest: reorder, skip, or see what's next

#### E. **Batch Wontfix Confirmation** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/selection.py`, lines 92-119)

**Guard**: Prevents silent bulk wontfix when > 10 items
- Estimate strict score impact (via state simulation)
- Require `--confirm-batch-wontfix` flag to proceed
- Warn with delta: "Estimated strict-score debt added now: X.X points"
- Useful for users to see cost of wontfixing large batches

#### F. **Triage Dependency Gates** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`, lines 494-592)

**workflow::* items are gated behind triage stages**:
- Required stages: `observe` → `reflect` → `organize` → `commit`
- If `workflow::create-plan` or `workflow::score-checkpoint` exists:
  - Must complete all 4 triage stages first
  - If missing: auto-inject triage stages into queue
  - Print targeted error: "You must analyze findings before resolving this"
  - Suggest: run next stage with specific command
- **Bypass**: `--force-resolve --note "reason" (min 50 chars)` + attestation

#### G. **Scan Gate** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`, lines 777-844)

**workflow::* items also gated on fresh scan**:
- Compares: `scan_count_at_plan_start` vs current `scan_count`
- If no new scan since plan creation:
  - Block workflow items
  - Suggest: `desloppify scan` OR `desloppify plan scan-gate --skip --note "reason"`

#### H. **--confirm Flag Pattern** (`cmd_plan_resolve`, line 500-506)

**Shorthand for plan-level resolve**:
- `--confirm` auto-generates attestation from `--note`
- Only works with `--note` present
- Generates: `"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."`
- Used in prompts/nudges: `desloppify plan resolve "<id>" --note "<what>" --confirm`

---

### 2. AGENT-FACING OUTPUT

#### A. **Output Formats** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/cmd.py` & `output.py`)

**NextOptions dataclass** (line 47-74):
```python
output_format: str = "terminal"  # default for human terminals
```

**Three output modes**:
1. **`terminal`** (default): Human-friendly ANSI-colored output
2. **`json`**: Full JSON payload (stdout via `emit_non_terminal_output()`)
3. **`md`**: Markdown table format

**Usage**:
```bash
desloppify next --format json  # Agent reads JSON from stdout
desloppify next --output /path/file.json  # Write JSON to file (with --format json implied)
```

#### B. **JSON Output Structure** (`output.py`, lines 98-141)

**Serialized item fields** (`serialize_item()`, lines 28-96):
- Common to all items: `id`, `kind`, `confidence`, `detector`, `file`, `summary`, `status`, `primary_command`
- Plan metadata (if present): `queue_position`, `plan_description`, `plan_note`, `plan_cluster`, `plan_skipped` (+ skip kind/reason)
- Blocking state: `blocked_by`, `is_blocked`
- Explain info: `explain` (if requested)
- Detail: full `detail` dict from issue

**Cluster items** get special serialization:
- `member_count`: total issues in cluster
- `members`: sample of cluster members (limited to 25 items via `_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT`)
- `members_truncated`: flag if sample limit exceeded
- `action_steps`: action_steps from plan (if cluster has them)
- `primary_command`: generated cluster action (e.g., `desloppify autofix ...` or `desloppify next --cluster NAME`)

**Top-level payload structure** (`build_query_payload()`, lines 98-141):
```python
{
  "command": "next",
  "items": [serialized items],
  "queue": {"total": count},
  "narrative": {dict},
  "overall_score": float,
  "objective_score": float,
  "strict_score": float,
  "scorecard_dimensions": [dimension entries],
  "subjective_measures": [subjective-only entries],
  "plan": {  # if plan active
    "active": bool,
    "focus": cluster_name_or_null,
    "clusters": [{"name", "description", "item_count"}],
    "total_ordered": int,
    "total_skipped": int,
    "plan_overrides_narrative": bool,
  }
}
```

#### C. **Query Logging** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/query.py`)

Each `desloppify next` call writes to `query.json` (via `write_query(payload)`):
- Persists payload for agent/CLI inspection
- Contains full queue state at that moment
- Used by tools to understand what was shown

---

### 3. QUEUE RENDERING PIPELINE

#### A. **Full `next` Command Flow** (`cmd.py`, lines 109-326)

**Key steps**:
1. `NextOptions.from_args()`: Extract user options (count, format, scope, etc.)
2. Load living plan (if exists)
3. Build **unified context** (`queue_context()`): Merges state, config, plan, target_strict
4. `build_work_queue()`: Core ranking/filtering engine (returns items)
5. **Collapse clusters** if no explicit cluster focus and plan present
6. Apply count truncation
7. Compute narrative
8. **Build payload** (`_build_next_payload()`)
9. Write query.json
10. **Emit output**: JSON/MD/file OR terminal render

#### B. **Cluster Collapsing** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`, lines 193-223)

**When clusters are collapsed** (lines 255-256 in cmd.py):
- If `not effective_cluster and not plan_data.get("active_cluster")`:
  - Call `collapse_clusters(items, plan)` → replaces cluster members with single meta-item

**Meta-item structure** (lines 140-191):
```python
{
  "id": cluster_name,
  "kind": "cluster",
  "action_type": cluster_data.get("action_type") or "manual_fix",
  "summary": cluster_description,
  "member_count": len(alive_members),
  "members": members_list,  # full member objects
  "cluster_name": cluster_name,
  "cluster_auto": True/False,
  "detector": detector,
  "primary_command": cluster_data.get("action") or f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name}",
  "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
}
```

#### C. **Terminal Rendering** (`render_support.py`)

**For regular items**:
- Effort tag: `[review]` or `[auto]` (if auto-fixable) or empty
- Line: `[confidence] {tag} {summary}`

**For cluster items** (`render_cluster_item()`, lines 126-158):
- Header: `(Type label, N issues{—optional?})`
- Summary line (yellow)
- Plan steps if present: `[plan: N steps] drill in to view`
- Files breakdown (up to 5 files shown, then "... X more")
- Sample of 3 members (ID only)
- Primary action command (cyan)
- Suggested commands for optional vs required clusters:
  - **Required**: resolve all, drill in, skip
  - **Optional**: skip, drill in, resolve all

---

### 4. CLUSTER ACTION GENERATION

#### A. **The Complete Pipeline** (`auto_cluster_sync.py`)

**Three stages** where actions are generated:

**Stage 1: Grouping** (`_group_clusterable_issues()`, lines 45-75):
- Filter: open, non-suppressed, non-manual issues only
- For each issue: compute grouping key via `_grouping_key(issue, meta)`
- Returns: `{key: [issue_ids], ...}` for clusters with ≥ 2 members

**Grouping keys** (`cluster_strategy.py`, lines 27-55):
- `review::dimension_name` → review detector + dimension
- `file::detector::basename` → structural/responsibility_cohesion + filename
- `typed::detector::subtype` → judgment-required detector + subtype
- `auto::detector` → auto_fix detector (no judgment)
- `detector::name` → fallback

**Stage 2: Description & Action Generation** (`sync_issue_clusters()`, lines 204-254):
- For each group (key):
  1. Extract detector meta
  2. Call `_generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)`
  3. Call `_generate_action(meta, subtype)`
  4. Create or update cluster with both

**`generate_action()` logic** (`cluster_strategy.py`, lines 131-154):
```python
if subtype and meta.fixers:
    # Check if subtype matches a fixer (e.g., "dead_import" → "dead-import")
    fixer = subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
    if fixer:
        return f"desloppify autofix {fixer} --dry-run"

elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
    # No judgment needed: use first fixer
    return f"desloppify autofix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"

elif meta.tool == "move":
    return "desloppify move"

elif meta.guidance:
    # Use detector's guidance, strip examples if subtype exists
    return strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance) if subtype else meta.guidance

else:
    # Fallback to action_type template
    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(meta.action_type, "review and fix each issue")
    # "reorganize" → "reorganize with desloppify move"
    # "refactor" → "review and refactor each issue"
    # "manual_fix" → "review and fix each issue"
```

**Stage 3: Persistence** (`_sync_auto_cluster()`, lines 137-193):
- Create new cluster dict with:
  - `"action"`: the generated action string
  - `"auto": True`, `"user_modified": False`
  - `"cluster_key"`: the grouping key (for dedup)
- Create override entries for each member: `overrides[issue_id]["cluster"] = cluster_name`

#### B. **Subjective Cluster Actions** (`sync_subjective_clusters()`, lines 257-379)

**Three special auto-clusters**:

1. **Unscored** (`auto/initial-review`):
   - IDs: unscored subjective dimensions
   - Action: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions dim1,dim2,dim3`

2. **Stale** (`auto/stale-review`):
   - IDs: previously-scored dimensions that haven't been re-reviewed recently
   - Action: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions ...`

3. **Under-target** (`auto/under-target-review`, optional):
   - IDs: dimensions below target strict score
   - Only created if NO objective items in queue (optional: `True`)
   - Action: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions ...`

---

### 5. SKIP OPERATION FLOW (Full Details)

#### A. **Skip Entry Schema** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`)

Each skipped item stored as:
```python
{
  "issue_id": str,
  "kind": "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive",
  "reason": str | None,           # optional, for temporary only
  "note": str | None,             # for permanent skip explanation
  "attestation": str | None,       # for permanent/false_positive
  "created_at": ISO timestamp,
  "review_after": int | None,      # temporary only: resurface after N scans
  "skipped_at_scan": int,         # which scan_count was active
}
```

#### B. **Skip Operation** (`skip_items()`, `operations_skip.py`, lines 12-43)

**Transactional logic**:
1. Validate input (in caller)
2. For each issue ID:
   - Remove from plan.queue_order (via `_remove_id_from_lists()`)
   - Create SkipEntry in plan.skipped[id]
   - Increment counter
3. Return count skipped
4. Caller handles state persistence (plan transactionally)

**Logging** (in `cmd_plan_skip()`, lines 270-278):
- `append_log_entry(plan, "skip", issue_ids=[...], actor="user", detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason, ...})`

#### C. **Unskip Operation** (`unskip_items()`, lines 46-67)

**Returns** `(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)`:
- For each ID:
  - Pop from skipped
  - If kind is `permanent` or `false_positive`: add to need_reopen list
  - Add back to end of queue_order
  - Increment count
- Caller separately handles state reopening (via `state_mod.resolve_issues(..., "open")`)

#### D. **Resurfacing Stale Skips** (`resurface_stale_skips()`, lines 70-93)

**Runs during plan load/operations**:
- For each temporary skip:
  - If `review_after` is set and current_scan >= (skipped_at + review_after):
    - Remove from skipped
    - Re-add to queue_order
    - Return list of resurfaced IDs

#### E. **State Coordination** (`cmd_plan_skip()`, lines 249-287)

**For permanent/false_positive**:
- Call `_apply_state_skip_resolution()` → updates state.issues[id].status
- Use `_save_plan_state_transactional()`: Write both state + plan atomically, rollback both if either fails

**For temporary**:
- Only plan changes (no state.status impact)

---

### 6. KEY DESIGN PATTERNS OBSERVED

1. **Params-driven design**: High-risk operations controlled via explicit flags, not implicit detection
2. **Transactional guards**: Plan+state writes guarded with rollback snapshots
3. **Validation layering**: Input validation → business logic → persistence
4. **Dual-keyword attestation**: Harder to game than single phrases (requires intent + awareness of gaming)
5. **Cluster action generation**: Detector metadata + subtype → deterministic command (autofix preferred, then guidance, then generic)
6. **Queue order enforcement**: Prevents silent out-of-order work when plan exists
7. **Batch operation warnings**: Score impact estimation shown before confirmation
8. **Triage gating**: Workflow items blocked until analysis complete (prevent premature closure)

This is the complete pattern foundation for agent operations, command generation, and CLI safeguards.
